结论: 在已知因果结构图、无环性及平衡分布的假设下，可以从横截面数据非参数地恢复扩散过程的完整因果漂移机制，估计量具有理论一致性保证。

局限 / 后续问题: 方法依赖于多个强假设：因果结构图需已知且无环、系统须处于平衡分布、扩散过程须时间齐次；实际应用中这些条件可能难以完全满足。

中文摘要: 我们研究在连续时间下依因果机制演化的稀疏多元随机系统，并提出从横截面数据中恢复系统时间无穷小转移机制的方法。这一观测范式由基因表达分析等应用驱动，此类应用中破坏性实验技术可能仅允许在细胞生命周期内记录一次数据。具体而言，我们假设系统服从时间齐次扩散过程，且在观测时刻已达到平衡分布。进一步，我们假设因果机制完全由扩散漂移项描述，具有无环结构，且其因果结构图已知。在此设定下，我们证明了在弱非爆炸准则下，完整的因果机制即漂移函数可被非参数地识别。我们针对这一具有挑战性的反问题推导了非参数核估计量并证明了其一致性。此外，我们提出了用于超参数调优的交叉验证方案，通过仿真展示了估计量的行为，并讨论了与不可逆生成扩散模型及低频采样数据之间的联系。

英文原始摘要: We consider sparse multivariate stochastic systems that evolve in continuous time according to a causal mechanism and present methodology to recover the system's time-infinitesimal transition mechanism from mere cross-sectional data. This observational paradigm is motivated by applications such as gene expression analysis, where destructive experimental techniques may only allow recording data once over a cell's lifetime. Precisely, we assume the system follows a time-homogeneous diffusion process that has reached an equilibrium distribution at observation time. Further, we assume the causal mechanism is fully described by the diffusion drift, is acyclic, and its causal structure graph is known. In this setting, we prove that the full causal mechanism, i.e., the drift function, can be non-parametrically identified under a weak non-explosion criterion. We derive a non-parametric kernel estimator for this challenging inverse problem and prove its consistency. Moreover, we propose a cross-validation scheme for hyperparameter tuning, illustrate the behavior of our estimator in simulations, and we discuss connections with irreversible generative diffusion models and low-frequency sampled data.

</details>

  <summary>更多信息</summary>

主要方法: 仅使用分布的矩、高斯代数和分块求逆，将神经网络参数平均的闭合情形与核散射问题的开放情形并置比较，给出完整的闭合情形对应词典，然后将开放情形的守恒通量账本框架试用于三个学习场景。

实验 / 验证: 在截断注意力图、token级传输算子和稀疏专家路由器三个对象上测试开放情形的输出，检验守恒通量账本是否能产生有意义的独有内容。

主要结果: 结果基本为负面：开放情形的独有内容要么缺失，要么是所选分割的人为产物，要么被训练目标钉在下限附近。操作上有用的不确定性是认知性的，属于闭合半边而非开放半边。

结论: 开放情形移植失败有结构性原因：它需要一个具有连续谱和波动型（非弛豫型）动力学的被消去扇区，而主流学习的有限或耗散对象无法提供。本文是札记而非结果，其价值在于精确定位该负面发现的结构性原因。

局限 / 后续问题: 本文自述为札记而非结果；开放情形的失败源于主流学习对象缺乏连续谱和波动动力学，这意味着在当前学习范式内难以克服；三个实验对象的开放性可能本身就不充分，或受训练目标约束而无法展现独有内容。

中文摘要: 对神经网络的随机参数进行平均与对一个高斯扇区进行边缘化是同一操作，即被消去块的Schur补；当该块闭合时，它返回一个协方差及其逆。网络集成所产生的正是这种闭合情形。开放情形是缺失的，而核反应理论已经给出了其完整理论。将散射问题投影到一组选定的通道上，其余通道将概率不可逆地输运到连续谱中，由此留下一个非厄米有效生成元，它精确地守恒并逐项记录其所损失的部分：即核光学模型及其广义光学定理。本文仅利用分布的矩、高斯代数和分块求逆（不涉及场论），将两种情形并置比较，并完整给出闭合情形的对应词典：神经正切核即Fisher灵敏度核，无限宽度高斯极限即高斯过程仿真器，惰性到特征的转变即降阶基仿真器的有效性边界。随后，我在截断注意力图、token级传输算子和稀疏专家路由器上测试了开放情形的输出，并报告了一个基本为负面的结果。守恒通量账本在开放性确实存在之处可以移植，但其独有内容要么缺失，要么是所选分割的人为产物，要么被训练目标钉在某个下限附近；而操作上有用的不确定性最终是认知性的，存在于对应的闭合半边而非开放半边。这一负面结果有结构性的原因，本文对此给出了精确表述：开放情形需要一个具有连续谱和波动型（而非弛豫型）动力学的被消去扇区，而主流学习的有限或耗散对象无法提供。本文是一篇札记而非一个结果；其主要发现即该负面发现，其价值在于定位该发现的映射图。

英文原始摘要: Averaging a neural network over its random parameters and marginalizing a Gaussian sector are the same operation, the Schur complement of the eliminated block, and when that block is closed it returns a covariance and its inverse. That is all a network ensemble produces, the closed case. The open case is missing, and nuclear reaction theory has it worked out. Projecting a scattering problem onto a chosen set of channels, with the rest carrying probability irreversibly to a continuum, leaves a non-Hermitian effective generator that conserves and itemizes exactly what it loses: the nuclear optical model and its generalized optical theorem. I set the two cases side by side using only the moments of a distribution, the algebra of Gaussians, and block inversion, no field theory, and give the closed-case dictionary in full: the neural tangent kernel is the Fisher sensitivity kernel, the infinite-width Gaussian limit is the Gaussian-process emulator, and the lazy-to-feature transition is the validity boundary of a reduced-basis emulator. I then test the open export on a truncated attention map, a token-level transfer operator, and a sparse expert router, and report a mostly negative result. The conserved flux ledger ports wherever openness is genuinely present, but its distinctive content is absent, an artifact of the chosen partition, or pinned near a floor by the training objective, and the operationally useful uncertainty turns out to be epistemic, living in the closed half of the correspondence, not the open one. The negative has a structural reason this note makes precise: the open case needs an eliminated sector with a continuous spectrum and wave-like, not relaxational, dynamics, which mainstream learning's finite or dissipative objects do not supply. This is a note, not a result; its main finding is that negative one, and its value is the map that locates it.

</details>

中文摘要: 本文研究了生物细胞中一种称为磷酸化的基本机制的稳定性性质。该系统是一个化学反应网络（CRN），其中一种化学物质，即底物，可以通过两类酶的活性被顺序转化为两种磷酸化形式，一类酶用于磷酸化，另一类用于去磷酸化。我们研究了该模型在质量作用动力学下的随机表示。底物的总质量固定为N，而酶的总质量与N成正比。研究了当N较大时所有化学物质浓度的渐近行为。我们研究了质量作用定律动力学下化学物质可能的稳定子集。稳定子集是指，在适当的初始状态下，当N变大时，该子集中物质的拷贝数在任何有限时间区间内保持O(1)。从这个角度研究了十二个反应速率常数（CRN的催化常数）的作用。建立了对应马尔可夫过程在CRN多个状态下的平均原理。特别地，证明了存在一个具有三个平衡点的状态，其中两个是稳定的。结果的证明依赖于泊松过程的随机微积分、马尔可夫过程坐标子集的适当耦合、关于M/M/∞排队的技术结果，以及R+^4中动力系统的稳定性分析。

英文原始摘要: In this paper we investigate the stability properties of a fundamental mechanism of biological cells called phosphorylation. The system is a chemical reaction network (CRN) for which a chemical species, {\em the substrate}, can be sequentially transformed into two phosphorylated forms, by the activity of two types of enzymes, one type for phosphorylation, the other for dephosphorylation. We investigate a stochastic representation of this model, under the mass action kinetics. The total mass of the substrate is fixed at $N$, while the total mass of enzymes scales proportionally to $N$. The asymptotic behavior, when $N$ is large, of the concentrations of all chemical species is studied.
  We investigate the possible {\em stable} subsets of chemical species for the kinetics of the law of mass action. A stable subset is such that, with a convenient initial state, the number of copies of the species of this subset remains $O(1)$ on any finite time interval as $N$ gets large. The role of the twelve reaction rate constants, {\em the catalytic constants} of the CRN, is investigated from this point of view. An averaging principle of the corresponding Markov process is established for several regimes of the CRN. It is shown in particular that there exists a regime with three equilibrium points, with two of them stable. The proofs of the results rely on stochastic calculus with Poisson processes, convenient couplings of subsets of coordinates of the Markov process, technical results on $M/M/\infty$ queues, and a stability analysis of a dynamical system in $\mathbb{R}_+^4$.

</details>

结论: 进化优化不仅提升预测性能，还揭示了循环基底上可解释的结构约束，稳定了适合任务的动力学类别并精炼了关键架构自由度，为研究预测需求如何塑造适应性动力学网络提供了仿生框架。

中文摘要: 生物系统通过将过去的刺激转化为支持前瞻性响应的内部动力学状态，在波动环境中维持功能。储备池计算提供了一个计算类比，但标准公式通常将循环基底视为固定的随机网络，仅训练读出层。在此我们提出：当储备池架构处于面向预测的进化选择压力下，基底本身会如何变化。以Kuramoto-Sivashinsky方程作为时空混沌的测试平台，我们在五个构造超参数上进化储备池：规模、连接度、谱半径、输入缩放和读出正则化。进化在群体层面降低了预测误差，延长了低误差预测时域，并沿一条边际递减的规模-效率前沿组织了设计空间。结构分析表明，进化后的储备池仍处于一个保守的随机块模型谱包络内，同时精炼了低特征值模式，将模块化锁定在中间频带，并在该频带内修剪连接成本。帕累托分析表明，精英储备池在成本-模块化平面上占据一个水平基面，表明准确性与效率是共同实现的，而非通过简单的权衡取舍。这些发现表明，进化优化不仅改善预测，还揭示了循环基底上可解释的结构约束：它稳定了适合任务的动力学类别，并精炼了与预测最相关的架构自由度。因此，进化储备池计算提供了一个仿生框架，用于研究预测需求如何塑造适应性动力学网络。

英文原始摘要: Biological systems maintain function in fluctuating environments by transforming past stimulation into internal dynamical states that support future-oriented responses. Reservoir computing provides a computational analogue, but standard formulations often treat the recurrent substrate as a fixed random network and train only the readout. Here we ask how the substrate itself changes when reservoir architecture is placed under evolutionary selection for prediction. Using the Kuramoto--Sivashinsky equation as a testbed for spatiotemporal chaos, we evolved reservoirs over five construction hyperparameters: size, connectivity degree, spectral radius, input scaling, and readout regularization. Evolution reduced prediction error at the population level, extended the low-error forecast horizon, and organized the design space along a diminishing-return size--efficiency frontier. Structural analyses showed that evolved reservoirs remained within a conserved stochastic-block-model-like spectral envelope while refining low-eigenvalue modes, locking modularity to an intermediate band, and pruning connection cost within that band. Pareto analysis showed that elite reservoirs occupied a horizontal floor in the cost--modularity plane, indicating that accuracy and efficiency were achieved jointly rather than through a simple trade-off. These findings show that evolutionary optimization does not merely improve prediction, but exposes interpretable structural constraints on the recurrent substrate: it stabilizes a task-suitable dynamical class and refines the architectural degrees of freedom most relevant for prediction. Evolutionary reservoir computing therefore provides a bio-inspired framework for studying how predictive demands shape adaptive dynamical networks.

</details>

主要方法: LRC-FNO包含四个核心组件：自编码器对粒子沉积源场进行压缩；潜在闭包精修器恢复压缩丢失的残差结构；粗粒度FNO求解器捕获主导场响应；残差闭包FNO恢复全分辨率修正。整体将场求解建模为两级残差闭包问题。

实验 / 验证: 在三个复杂度递增的基准上测试：1D线性朗道阻尼（LLD）、2D双流不稳定性（TSI）、2D刮削层（SOL）聚变等离子体模型。评估了单步预测精度和闭环长时间模拟的物理一致性。

主要结果: 在LLD和TSI中更好保持了电荷-电势映射、电势模式演化、残余电荷结构和粒子-场能量交换；SOL算例单步预测中自洽电势相对L2误差为0.0447，磁矢势为0.0251；作为神经初始猜测配合20次迭代修正时，在接近训练时长两倍的外推闭环模拟中保持强物理一致性。

结论: LRC-FNO既可作为快速代理场求解器，也可作为迭代PIC场求解器的高质量初始化策略，在精度和长时间物理一致性方面均表现优异。

局限 / 后续问题: 

中文摘要: 粒子模拟（PIC）在能源应用的动理学等离子体建模中被广泛使用，但其效率常常受到在密集网格上反复进行场求解的限制。本文提出了一种潜在残差闭包傅里叶神经算子（LRC-FNO），用于PIC模拟中稳健的代理多场求解。LRC-FNO不将场预测视为纯数据驱动的回归任务，而是将PIC场求解表述为一个涉及源场压缩和源到场算子映射的两级残差闭包问题。自编码器提取粒子沉积源场的紧凑表示，而潜在闭包精修器恢复在压缩过程中丢失的未解析残差结构。粗粒度FNO求解器捕获主导场响应，残差闭包FNO恢复全分辨率修正。该方法在三个复杂度递增的基准测试上进行了验证：一维线性朗道阻尼（LLD）、二维双流不稳定性（TSI）以及二维刮削层（SOL）聚变等离子体模型。在LLD和TSI中，LRC-FNO在闭环PIC积分过程中更好地保持了电荷到电势的映射、电势模式演化、残余电荷结构以及粒子-场能量交换。在SOL算例中，LRC-FNO在单步预测中自洽电势的相对L2误差为0.0447，磁矢势的相对L2误差为0.0251。更重要的是，当作为神经初始猜测并结合20次迭代修正使用时，LRC-FNO在外推闭环模拟中保持了强物理一致性，在接近训练时长两倍的时间范围内保持了电荷和电流密度结构。这些结果表明，LRC-FNO既可以作为快速代理场求解器，也可以作为迭代PIC场求解器的高质量初始化策略。

英文原始摘要: Particle-in-cell (PIC) simulations are widely used for kinetic plasma modeling in energy applications, but their efficiency is often limited by repeated field solves on dense meshes. This work proposes a Latent Residual-Closure Fourier Neural Operator (LRC-FNO) for robust surrogate multi-field solving in PIC simulations. Rather than treating field prediction as a purely data-driven regression task, LRC-FNO formulates PIC field solving as a two-level residual-closure problem involving source compression and source-to-field operator mapping. An autoencoder extracts compact representations of particle-deposited source fields, while a Latent Closure Refiner recovers unresolved residual structures lost during compression. A Coarse-FNO Solver captures the dominant field response, and a Residual-Closure FNO restores full-resolution corrections. The method is tested on three benchmarks with increasing complexity: 1D linear Landau damping (LLD), 2D two-stream instability (TSI), and a 2D scrape-off layer (SOL) fusion plasma model. In LLD and TSI, LRC-FNO better preserves charge-to-potential mapping, potential-mode evolution, residual charge structures, and particle-field energy exchange during closed-loop PIC integration. In the SOL case, LRC-FNO achieves relative L2 errors of 0.0447 for the self-consistent potential and 0.0251 for the magnetic vector potential in single-step prediction. More importantly, when used as a neural initial guess with 20 iterative corrections, LRC-FNO maintains strong physical consistency in extrapolated closed-loop simulations, preserving charge and current density structures over a time range close to twice the training horizon. These results demonstrate that LRC-FNO can serve as both a fast surrogate field solver and a high-quality initialization strategy for iterative PIC field solvers.

</details>

<details>
  <summary>更多信息</summary>

研究动机: 理解神经网络过度依赖虚假相关性的精确机制，为现有缓解算法所依赖的假设提供理论依据，填补虚假特征学习理论分析的空白。

主要方法: 在高维布尔超立方体数据上，设定XOR二次信号函数与线性虚假相关性，对两层ReLU网络经在线小批量SGD优化逻辑损失的过程进行理论分析，刻画虚假与信号特征的学习动力学及相变行为。

实验 / 验证: 在虚假相关性强度为常数的设定下，提供了初步的实验证据验证理论预测，表明模型最终能学习到XOR信号但虚假特征未被遗忘。

主要结果: SGD优先且指数级快速学习虚假特征；虚假与信号特征在优化中耦合，虚假分量越强越抑制信号学习；存在两阶段相变——第一阶段虚假特征符号与第二层权重对齐驱动快速增长，第二阶段多数群体大间隔减缓学习并持续抑制信号；虚假相关性最强时，即使达到独立学习XOR的样本复杂度阈值，虚假特征仍占主导。

结论: 虚假特征在SGD训练中被优先学习并指数级增长，通过耦合机制抑制真实信号学习，即使在足够样本量下虚假相关性最强时仍占主导；常数相关性下信号最终可被学习但虚假特征不会被遗忘。

局限 / 后续问题: 理论分析仅限于两层ReLU网络、XOR信号和线性虚假相关性的特定设定；常数相关性下的结论仅有初步实验证据；未考虑更深网络结构或其他信号函数的推广。

中文摘要: 神经网络已知容易过度依赖虚假相关性。然而，模型利用捷径特征的精确机制尚未被完全理解，且缓解该行为的算法依赖于对所学表征的尚未被证实的假设。在本工作中，我们首次对通过在线小批量SGD在逻辑损失上训练的两层ReLU神经网络的虚假特征学习提供了端到端的理论刻画。我们考虑从高维布尔超立方体中抽取的数据，具有二次信号函数（即XOR）和线性虚假相关性。我们证明SGD首先学习虚假特征，且速度呈指数级增长。此外，优化动力学将虚假特征和信号特征耦合在一起，较强的虚假分量会抑制信号特征的学习。我们的分析揭示了学习动力学中的精确相变。在第一阶段，虚假特征符号与第二层权重符号之间的对齐驱动了虚假特征的快速增长。在第二阶段，大多数群体的较大间隔 slows 学习，信号特征持续被抑制。当虚假相关性达到最强时，我们从理论上证明，即使在校立学习XOR所需的样本复杂度阈值处（即虚假特征不存在时），虚假特征仍然占主导地位。相反，当相关性强度为常数时，我们提供了初步的实验证据表明模型最终可以学习到XOR信号，尽管虚假特征并未被遗忘。

英文原始摘要: Neural networks are known to be susceptible to over-reliance on spurious correlations. However, the precise mechanism by which models exploit shortcut features is not fully understood, and algorithms to mitigate this behavior rely on as yet unjustified assumptions about the learned representations. In this work, we provide the first end-to-end theoretical characterization of spurious feature learning for two-layer ReLU neural networks trained by online minibatch SGD on the logistic loss. We consider data drawn from the high-dimensional Boolean hypercube with a quadratic signal function (namely XOR) and a linear spurious correlation. We show that SGD learns the spurious feature first, and exponentially fast. Moreover, the optimization dynamics couple the spurious and signal features, with a stronger spurious component inhibiting signal feature learning. Our analysis reveals precise phase transitions in the learning dynamics. In the first phase, alignment between the signs of the spurious feature and second-layer weight drives rapid growth of the spurious feature. In the second phase, large majority group margin slows learning and the signal feature remains suppressed. When the spurious correlation is maximally strong, we show theoretically that the spurious feature dominates even at the sample complexity threshold where XOR would be learned in isolation (i.e., if the spurious feature was absent). In contrast, when the correlation strength is constant, we provide preliminary empirical evidence that the model can eventually learn the XOR signal, although the spurious feature is not forgotten.

</details>

主要结果: 仿真中相比现有方法显著改善密集循环系统的结构恢复；fMRI数据中揭示了边级平均无法检测到的可重复大规模循环组织。

局限 / 后续问题: 

中文摘要: 大多数用于有向交互的统计和机器学习方法关注变量间的成对效应。即使现有的循环模型也主要通过节点级依赖来表示反馈，使得大规模循环组织难以估计和比较。这一局限性在生物和神经系统中尤为突出，因为这些系统中的交互高度循环且涉及许多重叠的循环。我们引入了一个用于循环交互统计推断的变分框架。有向交互被表示为单纯复形上的边流，并在能量最小化动力系统下演化。由此产生的动力学将瞬态交互分量与持续的谐波流分离，产生一个低维循环空间来捕获稳定的循环组织。该框架不枚举单个循环，而是将循环交互表示为希尔伯特空间中的元素，从而实现投影、平均、比较和群体级统计推断。我们建立了谐波投影的理论性质，包括循环空间的刻画、方差缩减和群体推断。仿真实验表明，与现有的有向交互方法相比，该方法在密集循环系统中显著改善了对循环结构的恢复。应用于400名人类受试者的静息态fMRI数据，该框架揭示了可重复的大规模循环组织，而这种组织通过边级平均无法检测到。这些结果为研究高维动力系统中的循环交互提供了一个可扩展的统计框架。

英文原始摘要: Most statistical and machine learning methods for directed interactions focus on pairwise effects among variables. Even existing cyclic models represent feedback primarily through node-level dependencies, making large-scale recurrent organization difficult to estimate and compare. This limitation is particularly acute in biological and neural systems, where interactions are highly recurrent and involve many overlapping cycles. We introduce a variational framework for statistical inference on cyclic interactions. Directed interactions are represented as edge flows on a simplicial complex and evolved under an energy-minimizing dynamical system. The resulting dynamics separate transient interaction components from persistent harmonic flows, yielding a low-dimensional cycle space that captures stable recurrent organization. Rather than enumerating individual cycles, the proposed framework represents cyclic interactions as elements of a Hilbert space, enabling projection, averaging, comparison, and population-level statistical inference. We establish theoretical properties of the harmonic projection, including characterization of the cycle space, variance reduction, and population inference. Simulations demonstrate substantially improved recovery of cyclic structure in dense recurrent systems compared with existing directed-interaction methods. Applied to resting-state fMRI from 400 human subjects, the framework reveals reproducible large-scale cyclic organization that is not detectable through edgewise averaging. These results provide a scalable statistical framework for studying recurrent interactions in high-dimensional dynamical systems.

</details>

结论: 所提框架在精度和计算效率上显著优于经典方法，能够在极低概率区间实现可靠的衰落持续时间分布估计和梯度优化，为稳健无线通信系统设计提供了有效工具。

局限 / 后续问题: 摘要中提及Markov投影降维仅在给定假设下成立，具体假设条件未详细说明；方法的适用范围可能受限于这些假设。

中文摘要: 表征无线信道中的衰落持续时间是设计稳健通信系统的基础。经典方法——Rice电平交叉理论和蒙特卡洛模拟——在尾部事件上缺乏精度，且在稀有事件概率估计方面计算代价过高。本文引入了一个严格的理论框架，结合Wiener混沌展开（WCE）、Malliavin变分 calculus以及具有自适应权重的重要性采样，用于分析衰落持续时间$Z(T)$的分布。主要贡献包括：通过WCE实现高精度矩估计和CCDF表征，最小化蒙特卡洛方差；Markov投影将无穷维动力学降维至可处理的系统（$\dim \leq 3$），适用于在给定假设下的Rayleigh、Rician和Nakagami模型；基于Malliavin敏感性导出的渐近最优重要性采样权重，实现839至2516倍的方差缩减；一种理论严谨且可证明高效的自适应SGD算法，采用Robbins-Monro步长调度进行参数估计。数值实验验证了本方法，相对误差低于0.5%，即使在$P \sim 10^{-15}$的区间内也能实现基于梯度的衰落持续时间统计优化，无需$\mathcal{O}(1/P)$量级的样本，而是通过解析Malliavin权重评估敏感性。

英文原始摘要: Characterizing fade duration in wireless channels is fundamental for designing robust communication systems. Classical approaches -- Rice's level-crossing theory and Monte Carlo simulation -- lack precision for tail events and are computationally prohibitive for rare-event probability estimation. This paper introduces a rigorous framework combining Wiener Chaos Expansion (WCE), Malliavin Calculus, and importance sampling with adaptive weights to analyze fade duration $Z(T)$ distributions.
  Main contributions include: (i) high-accuracy moment estimation and CCDF characterization via WCE minimizing Monte Carlo variance; (ii) Markovian projection reducing infinite-dimensional dynamics to tractable systems ($\dim \leq 3$) for Rayleigh, Rician, and Nakagami models under stated assumptions; (iii) asymptotically optimal importance sampling weights derived from Malliavin sensitivities, achieving 839 to 2516x variance reductions; (iv) a theoretically grounded and provably efficient adaptive SGD algorithm with Robbins-Monro step size schedule for parameter estimation. Numerical experiments validate our approach with relative errors below 0.5\%, enabling gradient-based optimization of fade duration statistics even for regimes where $P \sim 10^{-15}$, without requiring $\mathcal{O}(1/P)$ samples, by evaluating sensitivities through analytical Malliavin weights.

</details>


### [50] [Comparing Deterministic and Stochastic Parameter Recovery Algorithms Applied to Chaotic Systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.18568v1)
*Ashley Wang,Elizabeth Carlson,Franca Hoffmann*

主分类: 数值计算与科学计算

来源: arxiv

期刊: 

DOI: 

免费全文: [arXiv abstract](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.18568v1), [arXiv pdf](https://arxiv.org/pdf/2606.18568v1), [Google Scholar search](https://scholar.google.com/scholar?q=Comparing+Deterministic+and+Stochastic+Parameter+Recovery+Algorithms+Applied+to+Chaotic+Systems++free+full+text+pdf)

研究问题: 在混沌系统的噪声数据条件下，如何有效评估和比较确定性数据同化与参数恢复算法相对于传统随机方法的性能表现。

主要创新点: 系统性地将确定性PR算法与确定性DA算法进行配对组合，并在不同噪声水平下与随机PR算法进行全面比较，揭示确定性方法在精度、稳定性和计算效率上的综合优势。


<details>
  <summary>更多信息</summary>

研究动机: 混沌系统中的数据同化和参数恢复是重要但具有挑战性的任务，尤其在噪声存在时算法选择缺乏明确指导，需要系统评估确定性与随机方法的性能差异。

主要方法: 使用半解析方法数值构造Lorenz '63和多尺度Lorenz '96混沌系统的合成数据并添加白噪声，然后分别应用确定性DA-PR算法组合与传统随机PR算法进行对比实验。

实验 / 验证: 在不同噪声水平下，对Lorenz '63和多尺度Lorenz '96两个混沌系统的合成数据进行了数据同化与参数恢复的数值实验，比较确定性与随机方法的准确性、稳定性和计算效率。

主要结果: 确定性PR算法与确定性DA算法的组合在不同噪声水平下均比随机PR算法更准确、更稳定；确定性PR方法计算速度更快、效率更高、计算开销更低。

结论: 确定性PR算法在噪声混沌系统的数据同化与参数恢复中优于传统随机方法，未来应进一步探索其在噪声环境下的全部潜力。

局限 / 后续问题: 仅基于Lorenz '63和Lorenz '96两个混沌系统进行验证，未涉及更广泛的动力系统；确定性PR算法在噪声条件下的全部潜力仍有待深入探索。

中文摘要: 本文探讨了在混沌系统噪声数据条件下，各种新型确定性数据同化（DA）与参数恢复（PR）算法以及传统随机算法的有效性。我们使用半解析方法数值构造了来自Lorenz '63和多尺度Lorenz '96混沌动力系统的合成数据，并添加白噪声。研究结果表明，在不同噪声水平下，确定性PR算法与确定性DA算法的组合在计算上总体比随机PR算法更准确、更稳定。此外，确定性PR方法展示了更高的速度和效率，所需的计算能力低于随机PR方法。这表明未来工作应考虑探索确定性PR算法在噪声存在条件下的全部潜力。

英文原始摘要: This paper explores the effectiveness of various novel deterministic and traditional stochastic data assimilation (DA) and parameter recovery (PR) algorithms given noisy data from chaotic systems. We use semi-analytic methods to numerically construct synthetic data from the Lorenz '63 and multiscale Lorenz '96 chaotic dynamical systems, adding white noise. Our findings show that, for different noise levels, deterministic PR algorithms paired with deterministic DA algorithms are shown computationally to be overall more accurate and stable than stochastic PR algorithms. Additionally, deterministic PR methods have demonstrated greater speed and efficiency, requiring less computational power than stochastic PR methods. This suggests that future work should consider exploring the full potential of deterministic PR algorithms in the presence of noise.

</details>

研究问题: 全尺寸神经算子在多查询科学工作流中存储、部署和评估成本高昂，而传统的先训练大模型再压缩的策略可能效率低下且性能损失不可控。

主要方法: 从归一化输出坐标下的经验均值预测器开始，依次在残差场上训练同族微型神经算子序列，每步校正通过验证集选择的收缩因子加权整合；框架适用于FNO、DeepONet和CNO等多种神经算子架构。

结论: 

局限 / 后续问题: 存在PDE和架构依赖的场景，残差Boosting无法抵消压缩带来的精度损失；框架的有效性在不同问题类型和算子架构间并不一致。

中文摘要: 神经算子被广泛用作偏微分方程（PDE）的代理求解映射，但在多查询科学工作流中，全尺寸模型的存储、部署和评估成本高昂。本文提出Operator Boosting，一种逐级残差学习框架，用于直接构建紧凑的神经算子代理，而非先训练大模型再压缩。该方法从归一化输出坐标下的经验均值预测器出发，依次在残差场上训练一系列同族微型神经算子，并通过验证集选择的收缩因子将每一步校正整合进来。我们以傅里叶神经算子（FNO）、DeepONet和卷积神经算子（CNO）为实例，在来自PDEBench、APEBench和The Well的一维、二维和三维PDE基准上将Boosting微型堆叠模型与全尺寸单体基线进行比较。在30个数据集-架构对中，21个展现出正向的平均精度提升，17个具有正向的置信区间，同时所有Boosting堆叠模型均将可训练参数量减少约72-95%。最优模型比较在10个已完成PDE基准中的7个上展现出经验Pareto改进，包括二维Navier-Stokes、浅水动力学、Darcy流、一维输运与反应系统以及三维可压缩Navier-Stokes。这些结果表明，Operator Boosting通常能改善神经PDE代理的经验精度-参数Pareto前沿，同时也揭示了残差Boosting无法弥补压缩损失的PDE和架构依赖性区域。

英文原始摘要: Neural operators are widely used as surrogate solution maps for partial differential equations (PDEs), but full-size models can be costly to store, deploy, and evaluate in many-query scientific workflows. This work introduces Operator Boosting, a stagewise residual-learning framework for constructing compact neural-operator surrogates directly, rather than training a large model and compressing it afterward. Starting from the empirical mean predictor in normalized output coordinates, the method trains a sequence of tiny same-family neural operators on residual fields and incorporates each correction through validation-selected shrinkage. We instantiate the framework with Fourier neural operators (FNOs), DeepONets, and convolutional neural operators (CNOs), and compare boosted tiny stacks against full-size monolithic baselines across one-, two-, and three-dimensional PDE benchmarks from PDEBench, APEBench, and The Well. Across 30 dataset-architecture pairs, 21 show positive mean accuracy gains and 17 have positive confidence intervals, while all boosted stacks reduce trainable parameter count by approximately 72-95%. Best-model comparisons show empirical Pareto improvements on 7 of 10 completed PDE benchmarks, including two-dimensional Navier-Stokes, shallow-water dynamics, Darcy flow, one-dimensional transport and reaction systems, and three-dimensional compressible Navier-Stokes. These results show that Operator Boosting often improves the empirical accuracy-parameter Pareto frontier of neural PDE surrogates, while also exposing PDE- and architecture-dependent regimes where residual boosting fails to offset compression.

</details>

主要创新点: 提出将预测轨迹发散性与宏观吸引子形态相统一的几何引导机器学习框架，通过庞加莱占据网格构建结构 closeness 矩阵，并利用偏最小二乘回归提取与有限时间李雅普诺夫谱校准的潜在几何分量。


<details>
  <summary>更多信息</summary>

主要方法: 通过样本外k近邻预测误差提取局部不稳定性尺度建立ML-FTLE估计器；将时间发散映射到庞加莱占据网格导出的结构 closeness 矩阵；采用偏最小二乘回归提取与经验有限时间李雅普诺夫谱校准的潜在几何分量，得到基于庞加莱的几何引导FTLE。

实验 / 验证: 将所提方法与解析QR-FTLE基准进行对比验证；测试结构相似性指数和Hausdorff距离在不同转换模式下的表现；在加性高斯噪声条件下评估宏观空间离散化的抗噪性能。

主要结果: 融合拓扑状态空间与预测发散系统性提升了连续转换追踪能力；结构相似性指数最优解析渐进阻尼；Hausdorff距离在相空间突变坍缩时表现极端鲁棒；宏观空间离散化作为拓扑正则化器在中等信号阈值下仍保持确定性特征。

结论: 该无方程框架为监测复杂非平稳系统中的结构性转换提供了高精度、抗噪声的诊断工具，能够有效追踪突变和渐进状态转换。

局限 / 后续问题: 

中文摘要: 在无控制方程的条件下从标量观测中检测瞬态混沌是非线性动力学中的一个基本挑战。我们提出了一种几何引导的机器学习框架，该框架将预测轨迹发散性与宏观吸引子形态统一起来，以追踪突变的状态转换。该方法通过样本外k近邻预测误差提取局部不稳定性尺度，建立ML-FTLE估计器，随后将这一时间发散性映射到由庞加莱占据网格的最小字典导出的结构 closeness 矩阵上。通过采用偏最小二乘回归，我们提取出一个与经验有限时间李雅普诺夫谱直接校准的潜在几何分量，得到基于庞加莱的几何引导FTLE。与解析QR-FTLE基准的对比验证证实，将拓扑状态空间与预测发散性融合可系统性提升连续转换追踪能力。结构相似性指数能够最优地解析渐进阻尼，而Hausdorff距离在相空间突变坍缩期间表现出极强的鲁棒性。此外，宏观空间离散化作为针对加性高斯噪声的鲁棒拓扑正则化器，即使在中等信号阈值下也能保持确定性特征。这一无方程框架为监测复杂非平稳系统中的结构性转换提供了高精度、抗噪声的诊断方法。

英文原始摘要: Detecting transient chaos from scalar observations without governing equations represents a fundamental challenge in nonlinear dynamics. We propose a geometry-guided machine learning framework that unifies predictive trajectory divergence with macroscopic attractor morphology to track abrupt regime shifts. The methodology extracts a local instability scale via out-of-sample k-nearest neighbor forecast errors to establish the ML-FTLE estimator, subsequently mapping this temporal divergence onto a structural closeness matrix derived from a minimal dictionary of Poincare occupancy grids. By employing partial least squares regression, we extract a latent geometric component calibrated directly to the empirical finite-time Lyapunov spectrum, yielding the Poincare-based geometric-guided FTLE. Validation against analytical QR-FTLE baselines confirms that fusing topological state spaces with predictive divergence systematically improves continuous transition tracking. The Structural Similarity Index optimally resolves gradual damping, while Hausdorff Distance exhibits extreme resilience during abrupt phase-space collapses. Furthermore, macroscopic spatial discretization acts as a robust topological regularizer against additive Gaussian noise, preserving deterministic signatures even at moderate signal thresholds. This equation-free framework provides a highly accurate, noise-resilient diagnostic for monitoring structural transitions in complex non-stationary systems.

</details>

中文摘要: 我们使用概率和随机分析方法，对由 Belavkin 方程支配的有限维量子平均场系统证明了迹范数混沌传播。粒子为密度矩阵，通过平均场哈密顿量相互作用，并通过独立的扩散观测通道进行连续监测。极限动力学是非线性矩阵值 McKean-Vlasov 扩散，其随机性源于局部观测记录，而耦合则通过确定性的平均态实现。主要结果适用于任意单粒子密度矩阵（包括混合态），并涵盖完美测量和非高效测量两种情形。在初始数据具有强张量化条件下，每个固定边际在紧时间区间上一致收敛到非线性极限滤波器的张量积，并给出显式的定量界。证明结合了纯化、完全观测膨胀、条件期望、相对熵以及相关 Zakai 方程的一致稳定性。在斜伴测量情形下，外部观测噪声从边际方程中消失，恢复了随机 BBGKY 层级。在仅具有置换不变初始态的边际混沌性条件下，我们通过该层级的迭代证明了固定边际的收敛性。

英文原始摘要: We use probabilistic and stochastic-analysis methods to prove trace-norm propagation of chaos for finite-dimensional quantum mean-field systems governed by Belavkin equations. The particles are density matrices, interact through a mean-field Hamiltonian, and are continuously monitored through independent diffusive observation channels. The limiting dynamics is a nonlinear matrix-valued McKean-Vlasov diffusion, random through its local observation record and coupled through the deterministic averaged state.
  The main result treats arbitrary one-particle density matrices, including mixed states, and both perfect and inefficient measurement regimes. Under strong tensorization of the initial data, every fixed marginal converges uniformly on compact time intervals to the tensor product of the nonlinear limiting filters, with an explicit quantitative bound. The proof combines purification, fully observed dilation, conditional expectation, relative entropy, and uniform stability of the associated Zakai equations. In the skew-adjoint measurement case, exterior observation noises disappear from the marginal equations and a stochastic BBGKY hierarchy is recovered. Under only marginal chaoticity of permutation-invariant initial states, we prove convergence of fixed marginals by an iteration of this hierarchy.

</details>


### [54] [PhysGuard: Fisher-Guided Gradient Projection for Sim-to-Real Neural PDE Surrogates](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.16602v1)
*Changjian Zhou,Junfeng Fang,Negin Yousefpour,Peng Wu,Bin Yan,Guillermo A Narsilio*

主分类: 数值计算与科学计算

来源: arxiv

期刊: 

DOI: 

免费全文: [arXiv abstract](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.16602v1), [arXiv pdf](https://arxiv.org/pdf/2606.16602v1), [Google Scholar search](https://scholar.google.com/scholar?q=PhysGuard%3A+Fisher-Guided+Gradient+Projection+for+Sim-to-Real+Neural+PDE+Surrogates++free+full+text+pdf)

一句话结论: 提出PhysGuard框架，利用Fisher信息矩阵保护物理关键参数方向，实现神经算子高效仿真到现实适应。

研究问题: 神经算子模型从仿真数据迁移到实验测量数据时存在精度下降问题，而标准微调在利用有限真实数据时会破坏预训练中学习到的物理相关核心表示；现有知识保持方法面向视觉/语言任务，无法直接适用于需要保护核心尺度物理结构的神经算子。

主要创新点: 利用经验Fisher信息矩阵识别物理关键参数方向，将微调更新限制在不干扰这些方向的子空间内；通过分层Gram矩阵公式实现大规模模型的高效计算，并以自适应阈值自动确定保护子空间大小。


<details>
  <summary>更多信息</summary>

研究动机: 视觉和语言领域的知识保持适应方法无法直接应用于神经算子，因为神经算子需要保护的是核心尺度物理结构而非语义或视觉特征，因此需要专门面向物理知识保持的适应框架。

主要方法: 在仿真数据上计算经验Fisher信息矩阵以识别物理关键参数方向；将微调更新投影到不干扰这些方向的子空间中；采用分层Gram矩阵公式实现数百万参数模型的高效计算；通过自适应阈值自动确定受保护子空间的大小。

实验 / 验证: 在四种神经算子架构和不同物理系统的基准测试上进行实验，包括频谱探针实验验证Fisher方向与低频输出结构的关联，以及与基线方法的对比评估。

主要结果: PhysGuard在大多数评估指标上优于基线方法。在严重域偏移场景下优势最为显著，低频误差最多降低32%，同时保持适应能力。频谱探针实验证实主导Fisher方向与低频输出结构强烈相关。

结论: PhysGuard通过保护物理关键参数方向，有效实现了神经算子从仿真到现实的适应，在严重域偏移下仍能显著降低低频误差并保持适应性。

局限 / 后续问题: 

中文摘要: 在仿真数据上训练的神经算子模型在应用于实验测量数据时，由于仿真与现实的差异（sim-to-real gap）而常常损失精度。使用有限的真实数据进行标准微调可以缩小这一差距，但也可能破坏预训练阶段学习到的与物理相关的核心表示。尽管知识保持适应方法在视觉或语言任务中已被广泛研究，但这些方法是否适用于架构和受保护知识根本不同的神经算子仍不清楚。神经算子需要保持核心尺度的物理结构，而非语义或视觉特征。我们提出了PhysGuard，一个用于神经算子精确仿真到现实适应的物理保持框架。具体而言，PhysGuard使用在仿真数据上计算的经验Fisher信息矩阵来识别物理关键参数方向，然后将微调更新限制在不干扰这些方向的方向上。分层Gram矩阵公式使得该方法对于具有数百万参数的模型依然高效，同时自适应阈值自动确定受保护子空间的大小。频谱探针实验表明，主导Fisher方向与低频输出结构强烈相关。在四种神经算子架构和不同物理系统的基准测试上的实验表明，PhysGuard与基线方法相比在大多数评估指标上表现优异。其优势在严重的域偏移下最为明显，与标准微调相比，低频误差最多降低32%，同时保持适应性。代码可在 https://github.com/ZhouChaunge/PhysGuard 获取。

英文原始摘要: Neural operator models trained on simulation data often lose accuracy when applied to experimental measurements due to the sim-to-real gap. Standard fine-tuning with limited real data can reduce this gap, but it may also damage the core physics-relevant representations learned during pretraining. Although knowledge-preserving adaptation has been widely investigated in vision or language tasks, it remains unclear whether these methods are suitable for neural operators whose architectures and protected knowledge are fundamentally different. Neural operators need to preserve core-scale physical structures rather than semantic or visual features. We propose PhysGuard, a physics-preserving framework for accurate sim-to-real adaptation of neural operators. Specifically, PhysGuard uses the empirical Fisher Information Matrix computed on simulation data to identify physics-critical parameter directions, then restricts fine-tuning updates to directions that do not interfere with them. A layer-wise Gram-matrix formulation makes this efficient for models with millions of parameters, while an adaptive threshold automatically determines the protected subspace size. A spectral probe experiment shows that the dominant Fisher directions are strongly associated with low-frequency output structures. Experiments on benchmark across four neural operator architectures and different physical systems show that PhysGuard performs strongly on most evaluation metrics compared to baselines. The benefits are most evident under severe domain shift, where it reduces low-frequency error by up to 32\% compared to standard fine-tuning while maintaining adaptability. Our code is available at https://github.com/ZhouChaunge/PhysGuard.

</details>

一句话结论: 证明非可逆Langevin动力学的随机梯度EM估计器满足中心极限定理，并给出反对称扰动降低波动的充分条件。

研究问题: 广义非可逆Langevin动力学的随机梯度Euler-Maruyama估计量在消失步长机制下的首阶涨落行为缺乏理论刻画，特别是是否存在系统性的方差降低机制尚不明确。

主要创新点: 在无偏随机梯度预言机和小步长结构假设下证明了经验平均量的中心极限定理，将极限方差通过Poisson方程刻画为算子形式，并推导出反对称扰动严格降低首阶涨落常数的充分条件，同时给出了二次哈密顿量和线性观测量的闭式公式。


<details>
  <summary>更多信息</summary>

研究动机: 非可逆Langevin动力学在采样和优化中具有潜力，但其随机梯度离散化方案在有限步长下的统计性质尤其是涨落行为缺乏严格的理论分析，需要理解反对称扰动能否系统性地降低估计方差。

主要方法: 基于小步长中心极限定理的结构假设和无偏随机梯度预言机，利用极限全梯度扩散的Poisson方程刻画极限方差，将其改写为算子形式以联系连续时间渐近方差，在标准算子理论假设下推导反对称扰动降低涨落常数的充分条件；对二次哈密顿量和线性观测量进行独立的高斯计算得到闭式公式。

实验 / 验证: 在基本示例、使用合成数据的贝叶斯线性回归以及使用真实数据的贝叶斯逻辑回归上进行数值实验，验证预测的高斯涨落行为并比较非可逆方案与可逆基线的均方根误差。

主要结果: 数值实验支持理论预测的高斯涨落，非可逆方案相对于可逆基线持续降低了RMSE；理论框架覆盖了免Hessian高分辨率动力学和梯度调整欠阻尼Langevin动力学的正定子类等增广状态示例。

结论: 非可逆Langevin动力学的随机梯度Euler-Maruyama估计量在消失步长机制下满足中心极限定理，反对称扰动可在充分条件下严格降低首阶涨落常数，理论与实验一致地验证了非可逆方案的方差降低优势。

局限 / 后续问题: 

中文摘要: 我们研究了广义非可逆Langevin动力学的随机梯度Euler-Maruyama估计量的首阶阶涨落。在针对小步长中心极限定理定制的结构假设和无偏随机梯度预言机下，我们证明了在消失步长机制下，时间跨度为步长平方倒数阶的经验平均值满足中心极限定理。极限方差通过极限全梯度扩散的Poisson方程来刻画。随后，我们将该常数改写为算子形式，将其与连续时间渐近方差联系起来，并在标准算子理论假设下，推导出一个充分条件，使得反对称扰动相对于可逆基线严格降低首阶涨落常数。我们还识别了直接被主定理覆盖的有界光滑预测观测量。作为有界测试函数机制之外的一项独立高斯计算，我们获得了二次哈密顿量和线性观测量的闭式公式。该框架涵盖非可逆Langevin动力学及增广状态示例，包括免Hessian高分辨率动力学和允许随机梯度的梯度调整欠阻尼Langevin动力学的正定子类。在基本示例、使用合成数据的贝叶斯线性回归以及使用真实数据的贝叶斯逻辑回归上的数值实验支持了所预测的高斯涨落，并表明非可逆方案相对于其可逆基线持续降低了均方根误差（RMSE）。

英文原始摘要: We study the leading-order fluctuation of stochastic gradient Euler-Maruyama estimators for generalized non-reversible Langevin dynamics. Under structural assumptions tailored to the small-stepsize central limit theorem and under an unbiased stochastic gradient oracle, we prove that the empirical average over a horizon of order the inverse squared stepsize satisfies a central limit theorem in the vanishing-stepsize regime. The limiting variance is characterized through the Poisson equation of the limiting full-gradient diffusion. We then rewrite this constant in an operator form that links it to the continuous-time asymptotic variance and, under standard operator-theoretic assumptions, derive a sufficient condition under which an anti-symmetric perturbation strictly reduces the leading-order fluctuation constant relative to the reversible baseline. We also identify bounded smooth predictive observables that re directly covered by the main theorem. As a separate Gaussian calculation beyond the bounded-test-function regime, we obtain closed-form formulas for quadratic Hamiltonians and linear observables. The framework covers non-reversible Langevin dynamics and augmented-state examples including Hessian-free high-resolution dynamics and a positive-definite subclass of gradient-adjusted underdamped Langevin dynamics that allow stochastic gradients. Numerical experiments on basic examples and Bayesian linear regression using synthetic data, and Bayesian logistic regression using real data support the predicted Gaussian fluctuations and show that the non-reversible schemes consistently reduce the root mean squared error (RMSE) relative to their reversible baselines.

</details>

*Chang-Sik Choi,Francois Baccelli*

主分类: 数值计算与科学计算

来源: arxiv

期刊: 

DOI: 

免费全文: [arXiv abstract](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.29433v1), [arXiv pdf](https://arxiv.org/pdf/2606.29433v1), [Google Scholar search](https://scholar.google.com/scholar?q=Dynamical+System+Characterization+of+Heterogeneous+Walker+Satellite+Networks%3A+An+Orbit-Aware+Stochastic+Geometry+Perspective++free+full+text+pdf)

一句话结论: 提出轨道感知随机几何框架，基于Walker点过程叠加建模多高度LEO星座并分析下行性能。

研究问题: 异构多高度LEO卫星星座具有复杂的时空结构，现有建模工具难以有效分析其下行链路性能。

主要创新点: 将多高度LEO卫星系统建模为多个Walker点过程的叠加，并表述为由初始条件和卫星及地球转速决定的动态系统，建立周期性与遍历性的理论联系。


<details>
  <summary>更多信息</summary>

研究动机: 多高度LEO星座的复杂时空结构缺乏合适的随机几何建模工具，需要同时考虑轨道特性和动态演化。

主要方法: 将系统建模为Walker点过程叠加的动态系统，根据转速的有理可公度性证明周期性，根据有理无关性证明遍历性，并利用不变测度建立时间平均与期望的联系，进而推导距离分布、SINR覆盖概率和遍历吞吐量。

实验 / 验证: 本文为理论分析工作，未涉及仿真或实测实验，主要通过数学推导获得解析结果。

主要结果: 推导了给定纬度下典型接收机的最近卫星距离分布、SINR覆盖概率以及下行遍历吞吐量的解析表达式。

结论: 所提出的轨道感知随机几何框架为Walker型异构LEO卫星网络下行链路性能分析提供了严格且易于处理的工具。

局限 / 后续问题: 框架局限于Walker型星座结构；遍历性结果依赖转速有理无关的假设；仅分析了下行链路场景。

中文摘要: 异构且特别是多高度低地球轨道（LEO）卫星星座表现出复杂的空间和时间结构，需要新的建模工具来进行性能分析。本文开发了一种轨道感知的随机几何框架，用于建模当今在不同轨道和不同高度上的LEO卫星。具体而言，我们将该系统表征为多个Walker点过程的叠加，并将其表述为由初始条件以及卫星和地球转速决定的动态系统。我们证明，当各转速为有理可公度时，所提出的卫星系统是周期性的。随后，我们证明当各转速为有理无关时，系统是遍历的，从而建立了系统时间平均值与其在不变测度下期望之间的理论联系。我们推导了给定纬度下典型接收机的最近卫星距离分布，并分析了典型接收机的信号干扰加噪声比（SINR）覆盖概率。随后，我们推导了到典型接收机的下行链路通信的遍历吞吐量。总体而言，所提出的框架为分析Walker型异构LEO卫星网络的下行链路性能提供了一个严格且易于处理的工具。

英文原始摘要: Heterogeneous and in particular multi-altitude low Earth orbit (LEO) satellite constellations exhibit complex spatial and temporal structures, which require new modeling tools for their performance analysis. In this paper, we develop an orbit-aware stochastic geometry framework modeling today's LEO satellites on various orbits and various altitudes. In particular, we characterize such a system as the superposition of multiple Walker point processes and formulate it as a dynamical system determined by an initial condition and the rotation speeds of satellites and Earth. We show that when the speeds are rationally commensurable, the proposed satellite system is periodic. Then, we show that the system is ergodic when the speeds are rationally independent, establishing a theoretical link between time averages of the system and the expectation of it under the invariant measure. We derive the nearest-satellite distance distribution of a typical receiver at a given latitude and analyze the signal to interference-plus-noise ratio (SINR) coverage probability of the typical receiver. We then derive the ergodic throughput of the downlink communication to the typical receiver. Overall, the proposed framework offers a rigorous and tractable tool for analyzing downlink performance in Walker-type heterogeneous LEO satellite networks.

</details>

*Zongren Zou,Théo Bourdais,Ricardo Baptista,Houman Owhadi*

主分类: 数值计算与科学计算

来源: arxiv

期刊: 

DOI: 

免费全文: [arXiv abstract](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.16219v1), [arXiv pdf](https://arxiv.org/pdf/2606.16219v1), [Google Scholar search](https://scholar.google.com/scholar?q=Graphical+conditional+generative+modeling+for+digital+twin+modeling++free+full+text+pdf)

一句话结论: 提出从数据中发现影响目标量完整条件分布的关键变量，构建简约可解释的随机代理模型。

研究问题: 数字孪生建模中不断增加变量和数据流导致模型复杂度无限增长，系统难以维护、验证和解释；现有方法通常仅关注条件均值，忽略了通过变异性、尾部行为等体现的随机依赖关系。

主要创新点: 通过识别影响目标量完整条件律（而非仅条件均值）的候选输入来发现关键变量，将条件生成建模与基于高斯过程的方差分析（核模分解）耦合，实现非影响力输入的迭代剪枝与可解释结构发现。


<details>
  <summary>更多信息</summary>

研究动机: 在随机、粗粒化或部分观测系统中，变量间的依赖关系可能通过变异性、尾部行为、多模态或不确定性的变化体现，而非确定性函数关系，因此需要关注完整条件分布而非仅条件均值来发现真正 influential 的变量。

主要方法: 框架结合条件生成建模（学习目标量给定候选输入的条件分布）与基于高斯过程的方差分析（核模分解），迭代剪枝非影响力输入并发现可解释结构。在控制场景中，代理模型被解释为学习的马尔可夫决策过程，同时识别状态、动作和记忆变量以使动力学有效马尔可夫化。

实验 / 验证: 在随机动力系统、缺失变量场景、偏微分方程控制、强化学习和经济数据等多个示例上验证了所提框架。

主要结果: 所发现的结构产生了可解释的随机代理模型，其下游性能与在完整变量集上训练的模型相当。

结论: 该框架能够从观测数据中有效发现简约的变量集，构建可解释的随机代理模型，在保持下游性能的同时显著简化模型复杂度，适用于控制、强化学习等多种场景。

局限 / 后续问题: 

中文摘要: 数字孪生建模（包括模型不确定性下的控制和数据同化）通常面临一个开放式的保真度问题：增加变量、数据流和时间尺度会无限增加模型复杂度，最终产生难以维护、验证、解释并用于压力或安全测试的系统。作为替代方案，可以寻求仅基于描述相关感兴趣量所需变量构建的简约随机代理模型。我们引入了一个框架，通过识别哪些候选输入影响目标量的完整条件律（而不仅是其条件均值），从观测数据中发现这些变量。这一区分在随机、粗粒化或部分观测系统中至关重要，因为依赖关系可能通过变异性、尾部行为、多模态或不确定性的变化来体现，而非通过确定性函数关系。该框架将条件生成建模（学习给定候选输入时目标的条件分布）与基于高斯过程的方差分析（通过核模分解）相结合，实现对非影响力输入的迭代剪枝和可解释结构发现。在控制场景中，所得代理模型可被解释为学习到的马尔可夫决策过程：该方法不仅识别转移模型，还识别使所学动力学有效马尔可夫化所需的状态、动作和记忆变量。在涉及随机动力系统、缺失变量、偏微分方程控制、强化学习和经济数据的多个示例中，所发现的结构产生了可解释的随机代理模型，其下游性能与在完整变量集上训练的模型相当。

英文原始摘要: Digital twin modeling, including control and data assimilation under model uncertainty, often faces an open-ended fidelity problem: adding variables, data streams, and time scales can indefinitely increase model complexity, ultimately producing systems that are difficult to maintain, validate, interpret, and use for stress or safety testing. As an alternative, one can seek parsimonious stochastic surrogate models built only on the variables needed to describe the relevant quantities of interest. We introduce a framework for discovering such variables from observational data by identifying which candidate inputs influence the full conditional law of a target quantity, rather than only its conditional mean. This distinction is essential in stochastic, coarse-grained, or partially observed systems, where dependencies may appear through changes in variability, tail behavior, multimodality, or uncertainty rather than through deterministic functional relationships. The framework couples conditional generative modeling, which learns the conditional distribution of the target given candidate inputs, with Gaussian-process-based analysis of variance (through kernel mode decomposition), which enables iterative pruning of non-influential inputs and interpretable structure discovery. In control settings, the resulting surrogate can be interpreted as a learned Markov decision process: the method identifies not only a transition model, but also the state, action, and memory variables needed to make the learned dynamics effectively Markovian. Across examples involving stochastic dynamical systems, missing variables, PDE control, reinforcement learning, and economic data, the discovered structures yield interpretable stochastic surrogates whose downstream performance is comparable to models trained on the full variable set.

</details>


### [58] [Universal Construction of Generalized Lyapunov Functions for Nonlinear Dynamical Systems Using Physics-Informed Neural Networks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.14174v1)
*Z. C. Tu*

主要创新点: 提出广义Lyapunov函数作为非平衡势的统一概念，统一了普通Lyapunov函数、Freidlin-Wentzell准势和Ao型势；并设计了无数据的物理信息神经网络框架，将Lyapunov不等式和弱散度尺度兼容条件直接嵌入损失函数进行求解。


<details>
  <summary>更多信息</summary>

结论: 该框架为非线性非梯度系统的势景观构建提供了一条实用且通用的途径，广义Lyapunov函数统一了多种已有势概念。

局限 / 后续问题: 

中文摘要: 标量势景观是理解动力系统稳定性与转变的最有用方式之一。然而，对于非梯度动力学而言，为具有递归结构的非线性流构建全局Lyapunov型标量仍然是一个主要障碍。我们引入了广义Lyapunov函数——一种沿确定性轨迹非增的标量函数——作为非平衡势的统一概念。普通Lyapunov函数、Freidlin-Wentzell准势和Ao型势均可作为其特殊代表被恢复。随后，我们提出了一种无数据的物理信息神经网络框架，将Lyapunov不等式和弱散度尺度兼容条件直接嵌入损失函数中。该方法在线性系统、Hopf正规型、van der Pol振子以及具有两个相连极限环的三维Hopf链环流上进行了测试。学习得到的景观与已有的解析基准一致，并将不变集揭示为低势或等势结构，为非线性非梯度系统的势景观构建提供了实用途径。

英文原始摘要: A scalar potential landscape is one of the most useful ways to understand the stability and transition of a dynamical system. For non-gradient dynamics, however, the construction of a global Lyapunov-type scalar for nonlinear flows with recurrent structures remains a major obstacle. We introduce the generalized Lyapunov function, a scalar function that is non-increasing along deterministic trajectories, as a unifying notion of nonequilibrium potential. Ordinary Lyapunov functions, Freidlin--Wentzell quasi-potentials, and Ao-type potentials are recovered as special representatives. We then propose a data-free physics-informed neural-network framework in which the Lyapunov inequality and a weak divergence-scale compatibility condition are directly embedded into the loss function. The method is tested on linear systems, the Hopf normal form, the van der Pol oscillator, and a three-dimensional Hopf-link flow with two linked limit cycles. The learned landscapes agree with available analytical benchmarks and reveal the invariant sets as low-potential or constant-potential structures, providing a practical route to potential-landscape construction for nonlinear non-gradient systems.

</details>

主要结果: 部署的神经闭合在圆柱流场中紧密再现了平衡态和高斯动理学熵分布，在未见工况下保持稳定；实现了显著的在线加速，同时保留了参考模型的宏观、高阶和熵代理结构。

结论: GPU原生学习型闭合是加速立方FP稀薄流动求解器的实用途径，能够在保留参考动理学模型物理结构的同时提供实质性在线加速。

局限 / 后续问题: 

中文摘要: 基于粒子的Fokker--Planck（FP）模型在滑移和早期过渡态气流区域中提供了直接模拟蒙特卡洛（DSMC）的高效动理学替代方案，但先进的立方FP闭合需要在粒子模拟循环内反复进行单元级矩评估和小规模稠密线性求解。本文开发并验证了一种GPU原生神经代理模型，用于替代粒子模拟循环内部的确定性立方FP闭合计算。训练后的权重通过批量CuPy运算直接评估，避免了在线部署期间的CPU-GPU数据传输。验证侧重于定量证据：组件级运行时分析、包含离线成本的盈亏平衡成本分析、守恒与稳定性诊断、每单元粒子数敏感性分析、直接时间平均系数审计，以及基于协方差的熵代理保真度检查。Couette流动被保留为一个紧凑的无量纲验证问题，而主要的内部流动验证是二维顶盖驱动方腔流，通过完整的模拟条件进行测试，包括在标称Kn=0.5和Kn=1.0下未见的中等稀薄工况。对于高超音速圆柱问题，在前驻点线和单元中心近壁气体层中执行了基于粒子-矩协方差的熵保真度审计。用于圆柱流场的同一部署型神经C/Γ闭合，在所报告的前驻点线和近壁气体区间上紧密再现了平衡态和高斯动理学熵分布；这些分布被用作相对精确FP/ML-FP审计。本研究确立了GPU原生学习型闭合作为加速立方FP稀薄流动求解器的实用途径，在保留参考动理学模型的宏观、高阶和熵代理结构的同时实现了显著的在线加速。

英文原始摘要: Particle-based Fokker--Planck (FP) models provide an efficient kinetic alternative to direct simulation Monte Carlo (DSMC) in slip and early transitional gas flow regimes, but advanced cubic-FP closures require repeated cell-wise moment evaluation and small dense linear solves. This work develops and validates a GPU-native neural surrogate that replaces the deterministic cubic-FP closure calculation inside the particle simulation loop. The trained weights are evaluated directly with batched \texttt{CuPy} operations, avoiding CPU--GPU transfers during online deployment. The validation emphasizes quantitative evidence: component-level runtime profiles, break-even cost analysis including offline costs, conservation and stability diagnostics, particle-per-cell sensitivity, a direct time-averaged coefficient audit, and covariance-based entropy-proxy fidelity checks. The Couette case is retained as a compact, dimensionless verification problem, while the main internal-flow validation is a 2D lid-driven cavity tested by complete simulation conditions, including unseen moderately rarefied cases at nominal $Kn=0.5$ and $Kn=1.0$. For the hypersonic cylinder, a particle-moment covariance-based entropy-fidelity audit is performed on the front stagnation line and in the cell-centered near-wall gas layer. The same deployed neural $C/Γ$ closure used for the cylinder flow fields closely reproduces the equilibrium and Gaussian kinetic entropy profiles over the reported front-line and near-wall gas bins; these profiles are used as a relatively exact-FP/ML-FP audit. The study establishes GPU-native learned closure as a practical route to accelerating cubic-FP rarefied-flow solvers, delivering substantial online speedups while retaining the macroscopic, high-order, and entropy-proxy structure of the reference kinetic model.

</details>

主要方法: 构建MZ-GNN框架，将粒子加速度分解为当前图和延迟图上的有限记忆展开项，每项由等变消息传递GNN参数化；模型在直接数值模拟（DNS）数据上训练，采用自回归方式进行时间展开预测。

主要结果: 实验表明记忆项对恢复间歇性重尾加速度统计至关重要；学习到的动力学准确再现了单粒子弥散、粒子对弥散统计及四粒子四面体几何等未在训练中施加的可观测量。

结论: MZ-GNN框架为数据驱动的湍流输运多粒子模拟提供了一条物理结构化、可扩展的路径，同时也为学习具有相关性和丰富对称性粒子系统的降阶动力学提供了通用模板。

局限 / 后续问题: 

中文摘要: 我们提出了一种Mori-Zwanzig图神经网络（MZ-GNN）框架，用于学习均匀各向同性湍流中示踪粒子的降阶拉格朗日动力学。该模型将粒子加速度表示为当前及延迟粒子邻域图上的有限记忆展开，其中每项记忆贡献由等变消息传递图神经网络参数化。通过构造，该架构尊重问题相关的物理对称性，包括置换等变性、伽利略不变性，以及旋转和反射下的等变性。模型在直接数值模拟数据上训练，以自回归方式展开，并在训练过程中未施加的可观测量上进行评估。我们表明，记忆对于恢复间歇性的重尾加速度统计特性至关重要，且学习到的动力学准确再现了单粒子弥散、粒子对弥散统计以及四粒子四面体几何。我们的结果为数据驱动的湍流输运多粒子模拟建立了一条物理结构化、可扩展的路径，并为学习具有相关性和丰富对称性的粒子系统的降阶动力学提供了模板。

英文原始摘要: We introduce a Mori--Zwanzig graph neural network (MZ--GNN) framework for learning reduced-order Lagrangian dynamics of tracer particles in homogeneous isotropic turbulence. The model represents particle acceleration as a finite-memory expansion over present and delayed particle-neighborhood graphs, with each memory contribution parameterized by an equivariant message-passing graph neural network. By construction, the architecture respects the relevant physical symmetries of the problem, including permutation equivariance, Galilean invariance, and equivariance under rotations and reflections. Trained on direct numerical simulation data, the model is rolled out autoregressively and evaluated on observables that are not imposed during training. We show that memory is essential for recovering the intermittent, heavy-tailed acceleration statistics, and that the learned dynamics accurately reproduce single-particle dispersion, pair-dispersion statistics, and four-particle tetrad geometry. Our results establish a physically structured, scalable route to data-driven multi-particle simulation of turbulent transport, and a template for learning reduced dynamics of correlated, symmetry-rich particle systems.

</details>

研究动机: 经验熵率在弱混沌动力学中的解释存在微妙性：Lyapunov指数消失且不变测度为无穷时，标准的熵率归一化可能无法有效刻画系统的亚指数不稳定行为，需要澄清其能力与局限。

主要方法: 针对一维无穷不变测度间歇映射（修正Bernoulli映射和Boole变换），在固定有限划分下分析经验单步条件熵的渐近行为，通过返回序列尺度上罕见转移的自信息贡献推导双项衰减估计，并与广义Lyapunov和进行对比。

实验 / 验证: 对修正Bernoulli映射和Boole变换进行了数值模拟，验证了系综均值衰减的双项估计以及自归一化涨落与归一化Mittag-Leffler律的一致性。

主要结果: 经验熵率渐近收敛为零，使标准熵率归一化对亚指数不稳定性失明；但有限时间信息和通过长层流相间罕见转移的对数因子保持信息量，自归一化涨落非平凡且与Mittag-Leffler律数值一致。

结论: 经验Markov熵在无穷测度弱混沌中具有明确的能力边界：熵率本身失效，但有限时间信息和作为一种依赖于划分的可计算度量，仍能有效刻画稀疏符号转移的亚指数不稳定性。

局限 / 后续问题: 

中文摘要: 经验熵率被广泛用于量化符号或时间序列数据的不可预测性，但在弱混沌动力学中其解释十分微妙，因为此时普通Lyapunov指数为零且不变测度为无穷。我们通过研究一维具有无穷不变测度的间歇映射中固定有限划分下的经验单步条件熵来处理这一问题。对于无穷测度弱混沌区域中的修正Bernoulli映射和Boole变换，我们证明了该逐步经验熵收敛于零。因此，通常的熵率归一化在渐近意义上对亚指数不稳定性失去敏感性。然而，有限时间信息量仍然具有信息价值。长层流相之间的罕见转移发生在返回序列尺度上，其经验自信息贡献了一个额外的对数因子。在所陈述的正则性和矩假设条件下，该机制给出了系综均值衰减的双项估计，并得到了数值模拟的支持。尽管原始熵率为零，自归一化涨落仍然非平凡，且在数值上与归一化的Mittag-Leffler律一致。与广义Lyapunov和的比较表明，相应的信息和并非Krengel熵估计量，而是一种可计算的、依赖于划分的有限时间稀疏符号转移度量。这些结果阐明了经验Markov熵在无穷测度弱混沌中能够以及不能测量什么。

英文原始摘要: Empirical entropy rates are widely used to quantify unpredictability from symbolic or time-series data, yet their interpretation is subtle in weakly chaotic dynamics, where ordinary Lyapunov exponents vanish and invariant measures are infinite. We address this issue by studying the empirical one-step conditional entropy for the fixed finite partitions considered below in one-dimensional intermittent maps with infinite invariant measures. For the modified Bernoulli map and the Boole transformation in the infinite-measure weak-chaos regime, we prove that this per-step empirical entropy converges to zero. Thus, the usual entropy-rate normalization becomes asymptotically blind to subexponential instability. The finite-time information sum, however, remains informative. Rare transitions between long laminar phases occur on the return-sequence scale, and their empirical self-information contributes an additional logarithmic factor. Under the stated regularity and moment assumptions, this mechanism yields a two-term estimate for the ensemble mean decay, supported by numerical simulations. Although the raw entropy rate vanishes, self-normalized fluctuations remain nontrivial and are numerically consistent with normalized Mittag-Leffler laws. A comparison with generalized Lyapunov sums shows that the corresponding information sum is not a Krengel entropy estimator, but a computable, partition-dependent finite-time measure of sparse symbolic transitions. These results clarify what empirical Markov entropy can, and cannot, measure in infinite-measure weak chaos.

</details>

*Seth Minor,Vanja Dukic,David M. Bortz*

中文摘要: 逆散射变换（IST）为推导孤立子动力学提供了标准的理论框架。传统上，此类推导属于解析性质，而非数据驱动。本文将IST的概念框架与弱形式系统辨识方法相结合，直接从观测散射数据中发现有效的孤立子动力学，无需假设散射方程的先验知识。该方法通过在散射域中运算，避免了通过临时曲线拟合对孤立波进行参数化，从而生成可解释的低维模型，且在扰动和近可积区域仍然有效。我们在由Korteweg-de Vries型浅水方程支配的合成数据和实验数据上展示了所提方法的性能，并恢复了与经典IST理论一致的模型。

英文原始摘要: The inverse scattering transform (IST) provides the standard theoretical framework for deriving soliton dynamics. Traditionally, such derivations have been of an analytical, rather than data-driven, nature. In this paper, we combine the conceptual framework of the IST with weak-form system identification methods to discover effective soliton dynamics directly from observed scattering data, without assuming prior knowledge of the scattering equations. Our method avoids parameterizing solitary waves via ad hoc curve-fitting by working in the scattering domain, yielding interpretable low-dimensional models that remain valid in perturbed and near-integrable regimes. We demonstrate the performance of the proposed approach on synthetic and experimental data governed by shallow-water equations of Korteweg--de Vries-type and recover models that are consistent with canonical IST theory.

</details>

  <summary>更多信息</summary>

主要方法: HAMNO结合局部卷积表示、全局谱算子和层次编码器-解码器处理，核心为数据相关门控机制在每个空间位置自适应平衡局部和全局信息。PI-HAMNO采用多目标损失策略：数据拟合项加上强形式项（物理坐标下全域PDE残差平方积分）和弱形式项（残差乘以有限元测试函数后用质心四面体求积计算单元积分）。

主要结果: HAMNO在预测精度上优于标准神经算子基线；PI-HAMNO在稳定性、物理一致性和数据效率方面进一步提升表现，尤其在数据受限和分布外场景中优势明显。

结论: 所提出的HAMNO和PI-HAMNO框架能够有效学习非线性时间依赖PDE的解映射，在多尺度特征分辨、长程依赖保持和长期演化稳定性方面优于现有方法，物理约束的引入进一步增强了模型的物理一致性和数据效率。

局限 / 后续问题: 

中文摘要: 神经算子为直接在函数空间中学习偏微分方程的解映射提供了强大的框架。然而，许多现有架构在表示涉及多尺度结构、长程交互和稳定长时间演化的非线性时间依赖系统时仍面临困难。在本工作中，我们提出了层次自适应多尺度神经算子（HAMNO），这是一种结合局部卷积表示、全局谱算子和层次编码器-解码器处理的神经算子架构。HAMNO的核心组件是一个数据相关的门控机制，能够在每个空间位置自适应地平衡局部和全局信息，使模型能够在保持长程依赖的同时分辨精细尺度特征。我们进一步开发了物理约束扩展PI-HAMNO，基于多目标损失策略，将数据拟合与强形式和弱形式物理约束相结合。强形式项在物理坐标下对全域积分的PDE残差平方进行惩罚，弱形式项则通过将控制方程残差乘以有限元测试函数并使用基于质心的四面体求积法计算所得的单元积分来构建。该框架在立方体域上定义的非周期Allen-Cahn（AC）、Cahn-Hilliard（CH）和Swift-Hohenberg（SH）方程上进行了评估。在长期rollout预测、数据受限训练、分布外初始条件偏移和随机种子变化等多种设置下，HAMNO相比标准神经算子基线提升了预测精度，而PI-HAMNO进一步增强了稳定性、物理一致性和数据效率。实现代码已公开于 https://github.com/MBamdad/HAMNO 。

英文原始摘要: Neural operators provide a powerful framework for learning solution mappings of partial differential equations directly in function space. However, many existing architectures still struggle to represent nonlinear time-dependent systems that involve multi-scale structures, long-range interactions, and stable long-time evolution. In this work, we introduce the Hierarchical Adaptive Multi-scale Neural Operator (HAMNO), a neural-operator architecture that combines local convolutional representations, global spectral operators, and hierarchical encoder-decoder processing. The central component of HAMNO is a data-dependent gating mechanism that adaptively balances local and global information at each spatial location, allowing the model to resolve fine-scale features while preserving long-range dependencies.
  We further develop a physics-informed extension, PI-HAMNO, based on a multi-objective loss strategy that combines data fitting with strong- and weak-form physics constraints. The strong-form term penalizes the domain-integrated squared PDE residual in physical coordinates, while the weak-form term is constructed by multiplying the governing residual by finite-element test functions and evaluating the resulting element integrals using centroid-based tetrahedral quadrature. The framework is evaluated on non-periodic Allen-Cahn (AC), Cahn-Hilliard (CH), and Swift-Hohenberg (SH) equations defined on cubic domains. Across long-horizon rollout, data-limited training, out-of-distribution initial-condition shifts, and random-seed variations, HAMNO improves predictive accuracy over standard neural-operator baselines, while PI-HAMNO further enhances stability, physical consistency, and data efficiency. The implementation is publicly available at https://github.com/MBamdad/HAMNO .

</details>

来源: arxiv

一句话结论: 利用可解释的CoBRAS降阶方法揭示极端事件机制，实现预测与抑制控制。

主要创新点: 提出基于自动可微框架反向传播的CoBRAS方法及其局部敏感性变体，避免传统伴随计算，并支持通过神经网络代理模型扩展至实验系统。


<details>
  <summary>更多信息</summary>

研究动机: 极端事件在混沌动力系统中普遍存在但难以预测，需要一种可解释的方法揭示其潜在机制，从而实现数据驱动的预测与事件抑制控制。

主要方法: 采用CoBRAS方法识别线性斜投影以捕捉感兴趣量的敏感性并重建状态，利用自动可微框架的反向传播替代伴随计算；引入CoBRAS局部变体处理空间局部化事件；通过学习神经网络代理模型扩展至非可微系统。

实验 / 验证: 在三个挑战性系统上验证：二维Kolmogorov流的湍流能量耗散爆发、耦合FitzHugh-Nagumo振子网络的自发同步、修正非线性薛定谔方程描述的海洋怪波局部化形成。

主要结果: 简单预测模型在所有示例中准确预测了极端事件，基于揭示的机制设计的控制律能够有效防止这些事件；神经网络代理模型成功将方法扩展至非原生可微系统。

结论: 所提可解释方法能有效揭示极端事件的潜在机制，实现准确预测与事件抑制，并通过代理模型扩展至实验和非可微系统。

局限 / 后续问题: 

中文摘要: 极端事件——如地震和日冕物质抛射——在许多混沌动力系统中普遍存在，但由于驱动它们的微妙失稳机制，难以被刻画和预测。在本工作中，我们开发了一种可解释的技术，能够揭示极端事件背后的潜在机制，并利用这些机制构建数据驱动的预测模型和直观的事件抑制控制器。具体而言，我们利用基于伴随快照的协方差平衡降阶方法（CoBRAS），识别最能捕捉感兴趣量敏感性的线性斜投影，并重建原始状态。重要的是，我们避免了对繁琐伴随计算的需求，转而通过现代自动可微数值框架中的反向传播来实现。为适应空间局部化事件，我们还引入了CoBRAS的新变体，以获得局部敏感性平衡投影。我们在一组具有挑战性的多样系统中展示了该方法刻画极端事件的效用，包括二维Kolmogorov流中能量耗散的湍流爆发、耦合FitzHugh-Nagumo振子网络中的自发同步，以及由修正非线性薛定谔方程描述的海洋怪波的局部化形成。对于每个示例，我们展示了简单的预测模型能准确预测极端事件，且其潜在机制可用于设计控制律以防止这些事件。最后，我们证明通过直接从数据中学习动力学神经网络代理模型，可以将该方法扩展至实验系统以及非原生以自动可微编程语言编写的系统。

英文原始摘要: Extreme events -- such as earthquakes and coronal mass ejections -- are common in many chaotic dynamical systems, yet are difficult to characterize and predict due to the subtle instability mechanisms that drive them. In this work, we develop an interpretable technique that reveals the underlying mechanisms behind extreme events and uses them to build data-driven forecasts and intuitive event suppression controllers. In particular, we utilize the covariance balancing reduction using adjoint snapshots (CoBRAS) method to identify linear oblique projections that best capture the sensitivity of a quantity of interest and reconstruct the original state. Importantly, we bypass the need for cumbersome adjoint calculations, instead using backpropagation via modern automatically differentiable numerical frameworks. To accommodate spatially localized events, we also introduce a new variant of CoBRAS to obtain local sensitivity-balanced projections. We demonstrate the utility of this approach to characterize extreme events across a diverse set of challenging systems, including turbulent bursts of energy dissipation in the 2D Kolmogorov Flow, spontaneous synchronization in networks of coupled FitzHugh-Nagumo oscillators, and the localized formation of ocean rogue waves from a modified nonlinear Schrödinger equation. For each example, we show that our simple forecast models accurately predict extreme events and that the underlying mechanisms may be used to design control laws to prevent these events. Finally, we demonstrate that by learning a neural network surrogate model of the dynamics directly from data, we may extend this approach to experimental systems and systems that are not natively written in an automatically differentiable programming language.

</details>

*Handi Zhang,Adrienne M. Propp,Brooks Kinch,Houman Owhadi,Nathaniel Trask*

主分类: 数值计算与科学计算

来源: arxiv

期刊:

一句话结论: 构建保结构降阶模型，结合混合有限元与高斯过程回归实现PDE实时求解与不确定性量化。

研究问题: 科学机器学习方法虽能近实时求解PDE，但缺乏传统模拟器所具备的理论基础来支撑验证与确认，无法保证物理守恒结构和不确定性量化。

主要创新点: 在混合有限元空间与高斯过程回归之间构建新接口，利用外微积分揭示的拓扑结构设计保结构H(div)-L^2子空间，并将训练表述为带守恒约束的最优恢复问题，实现快速Schur补训练。


<details>
  <summary>更多信息</summary>

研究动机: 现有科学机器学习方法缺乏传统模拟器的理论基础，需要构建兼具实时性、物理守恒结构和不确定性量化能力的代理模型。

主要方法: 提出由轻量级Transformer指定的Raviart-Thomas和dgP_0元素的保结构H(div)-L^2子空间；用GP描述体积间通量的守恒律学习降阶动力学；将训练表述为带等式约束的最优恢复问题，采用Schur补快速求解；训练后以闭式估计器实时求解边界通量。

实验 / 验证: 数值实验验证了后验分布作为误差估计代理的准确性，并提供了线性泛函的RKHS后验误差界以支持不确定性量化。

主要结果: 训练后的模型可实时求解边界通量，后验分布在数值实验中作为误差估计代理表现出准确性，且具有闭式不确定性表达式。

结论: 所提出的保结构降阶模型通过混合有限元与GP回归的新接口，在保持物理守恒结构的同时实现了PDE的实时求解与不确定性量化。

局限 / 后续问题: 后验误差界仅针对线性泛函，非线性情形的理论保证尚未涉及。

中文摘要: 科学机器学习的最新进展为偏微分方程（PDEs）提供了近实时求解手段，但缺乏传统模拟器所具备的支撑当代验证与确认的理论基础。在本工作中，我们构建了数据驱动的降阶模型，作为保结构实时代理模型。值得注意的是，施加物理守恒结构的外微积分同时也揭示了拓扑结构，我们利用该拓扑结构构建了状态-通量关系中不确定性的高斯过程（GP）表示，最终得到感兴趣量的Dirichlet-to-Neumann映射，并具有后验不确定性的闭式表达式。我们特别提出了由轻量级Transformer指定的传统Raviart-Thomas和dgP_0元素的保结构H(div)-L^2子空间。通过提出一个以GP描述体积间通量的守恒律，学习与该子空间一致的降阶动力学。本工作的核心在于混合有限元空间与GP回归之间的新接口；当训练被表述为最优恢复问题（ORP）时，所得的GP回归可以写成带有施加守恒结构的等式约束的优化问题，适用于快速的Schur补训练策略。训练后的模型可以通过由给定Dirichlet数据驱动的边界通量闭式估计器实时求解。本文还包含线性泛函的RKHS后验误差界以支持不确定性量化，以及数值实验，证明了后验分布作为误差估计代理的准确性。

英文原始摘要: Recent advances in scientific machine learning provide a means of near-real-time solution to partial differential equations (PDEs), but lack the theoretical underpinnings of conventional simulators that support contemporary verification and validation. In this work, we construct data-driven reduced-order models that serve as structure-preserving, real-time surrogates. Remarkably, the exterior calculus that imposes physical conservation structure also exposes topological structure that we use to build a Gaussian process (GP) representation of uncertainty in state-flux relationships, ultimately yielding a Dirichlet-to-Neumann map for quantities of interest with closed-form expressions for posterior uncertainty. We specifically propose structure-preserving $H(\mathrm{div})$--$L^2$ subspaces of conventional Raviart--Thomas and $dgP_0$ elements prescribed by a lightweight transformer. Reduced-order dynamics consistent with this subspace are learned by posing a conservation law in which a GP describes the fluxes between volumes. This work hinges on a novel interface between mixed FEM spaces and GP regression; when training is posed as the optimal recovery problem (ORP), the resulting GP regression can be written as an optimization problem with equality constraints that impose a conservation structure, amenable to a fast Schur-complement training strategy. The trained model can then be solved in real time with closed-form estimators for boundary fluxes driven by prescribed Dirichlet data. The paper includes RKHS posterior error bounds for linear functionals to support uncertainty quantification, as well as numerical experiments demonstrating the accuracy of the posterior distribution as a surrogate for error estimation.

</details>


### [66] [Lagrangian Ellipsoid Diagnostics for Stochastic Hydrodynamics: Source--Sink Modeling of Deforming Particle Clouds](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.27606v1)
*Michael Chertkov*

主分类: 数值计算与科学计算

来源: arxiv

期刊: 

DOI: 

免费全文: [arXiv abstract](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.27606v1), [arXiv pdf](https://arxiv.org/pdf/2605.27606v1), [Google Scholar search](https://scholar.google.com/scholar?q=Lagrangian+Ellipsoid+Diagnostics+for+Stochastic+Hydrodynamics%3A+Source--Sink+Modeling+of+Deforming+Particle+Clouds++free+full+text+pdf)

一句话结论: 提出Löwner-John变形云方案，实现随机流拉格朗日诊断与可解释降阶建模。

研究问题: 缺乏有效且可解释的拉格朗日诊断方法来分析具有惯性区间的不可压缩随机流中粒子云变形的尺度依赖动力学行为，并将观测数据转化为可解释的有限维随机动力学模型。

主要创新点: 将Löwner-John最小体积外接椭球与粗粒化速度梯度结合作为拉格朗日诊断量，并将降阶建模表述为物理信息驱动的生成器辨识；通过白箱闭合将纵横比饱和解释为应变对齐与几何松弛的平衡，而非单纯拟合。


<details>
  <summary>更多信息</summary>

研究动机: 需要从粒子云数据中提取可解释的有限维随机动力学描述，以服务于湍流等复杂流动应用，同时理解变形饱和的物理机制。

主要方法: 在紫外尺度释放充满体积的粒子云，以最小体积外接椭球的惯性张量和椭球上粗粒化速度梯度作为诊断量；在二维各向同性不可压缩Gauss-Hölder有限时间相关速度场（Ornstein-Uhlenbeck Fourier模式谱生成）上测试；将纵横比动力学分解为对齐应变源和Löwner-John残差；构建白箱闭合模型，包括尺度依赖随机驱动项、von-Mises偏置对齐表示和仿射反馈残差闭合。

实验 / 验证: 在具有Kolmogorov指数的二维各向同性不可压缩Gauss-Hölder有限时间相关速度场上测试Löwner-John变形云方案，分析粒子云经验序列的统计特征。

主要结果: 椭球纵横比广泛波动但统计饱和；感知梯度呈现明确尺度依赖性；应变-涡量平衡近似处于普通张量层级；纵横比饱和被成功解释为持续应变对齐与几何松弛的平衡。

结论: 该方案提供了从粒子云数据到可解释有限维随机动力学的可移植路径，纵横比饱和的物理机制得到了解释而非仅拟合，为未来湍流应用奠定了基础。

局限 / 后续问题: 仅在二维合成Gauss-Hölder速度场上测试，尚未推广至真实三维湍流；降阶模型的可移植性和有效性在更复杂流动条件下仍有待验证。

中文摘要: 我们提出Löwner-John变形云方案，作为具有惯性区间的不可压缩随机流的拉格朗日诊断方法。在紫外尺度释放充满体积的粒子云，并在每个时刻用两个对象进行概括：其最小体积外接椭球的惯性张量以及在该椭球上粗粒化的速度梯度。我们在具有Kolmogorov指数的二维各向同性不可压缩Gauss-Hölder有限时间相关速度场上测试该方案，该速度场通过Ornstein-Uhlenbeck Fourier模式谱方法生成。所得的经验序列显示出广泛波动但统计饱和的椭球纵横比、感知梯度的明确尺度依赖性，以及近似普通的张量层级应变-涡量平衡。随后，我们将该序列的降阶建模表述为物理信息驱动的生成器辨识。在描述尺度、纵横比、应变幅值、涡量和应变-椭球对齐的内在变量中，纵横比动力学分离为对齐应变源和Löwner-John残差。最终的白箱闭合将应变和涡量建模为尺度依赖的随机驱动项，用平稳von-Mises偏置表示对齐，并通过尺度依赖的仿射反馈闭合残差。因此，观测到的纵横比饱和不仅是被拟合的，而是被解释为持续应变对齐与外接椭球几何松弛之间的平衡。该构造为未来湍流应用提供了从粒子云数据到可解释有限维随机动力学的可移植路径。

英文原始摘要: We propose the Lowner--John deform-cloud scheme as a Lagrangian diagnostic for incompressible stochastic flows with an inertial range. A volume-filled particle cloud is released at the ultraviolet scale and summarized at each time by two objects: the inertia tensor of its minimum-volume enclosing ellipsoid and the velocity gradient coarse-grained over that ellipsoid. We test the scheme on a two-dimensional isotropic incompressible Gaussian--Holder finite-time-correlated velocity field with Kolmogorov exponent, generated spectrally with Ornstein--Uhlenbeck Fourier modes. The resulting empirical train shows a broadly fluctuating but statistically saturated ellipsoid aspect ratio, a clear scale dependence of the perceived gradient, and an approximately ordinary tensor-level strain--vorticity balance.
  We then formulate reduced modeling of the train as physics-informed generator identification. In intrinsic variables describing scale, aspect ratio, strain amplitude, vorticity, and strain--ellipsoid alignment, the aspect-ratio dynamics separates into an aligned-strain source and a Lowner--John residual. The final open-box closure models strain and vorticity as scale-dependent stochastic drivers, represents alignment by a stationary von--Mises bias, and closes the residual by a scale-dependent affine feedback. Thus the observed aspect-ratio saturation is not merely fitted; it is explained as a balance between persistent strain alignment and geometric relaxation of the enclosing ellipsoid. The construction provides a portable route from particle-cloud data to interpretable finite-dimensional stochastic dynamics for future turbulent-flow applications.

</details>

研究问题: 在混沌动力系统的长期预测中，不同深度神经网络架构对预测稳定性的影响缺乏公平、系统的比较与评估。

主要创新点: 在统一训练协议和匹配模型容量下，结合局部雅可比矩阵、相对单步偏差、有限时间Lyapunov增长、吸引子分析及消融实验等多维度指标，系统量化不同架构在长期推演中的误差传播与几何复现能力差异。


<details>
  <summary>更多信息</summary>

研究动机: 混沌动力系统的高保真模拟计算成本高昂，代理模型虽有效，但不同架构的长期预测稳定性差异尚不明确，需要公平评估以指导架构选择。

主要方法: 在统一训练协议下比较多种常用深度神经网络架构，匹配模型容量进行实验，同时对各模型单独优化的情景进行分析；采用逐步误差注入、扰动放大分析、吸引子分析和消融实验等方法进行多维度评估。

实验 / 验证: 在双摆、Kuramoto-Sivashinsky方程和Kolmogorov流三个混沌系统上进行测试，分别考察匹配容量和单独优化两种情景下的长期推演表现。

主要结果: 不同架构在长期推演中表现出类别性差异；具有积分器式更新的模型在偏差、扰动放大和系统几何复现方面均优于其他架构，长期推演更稳定且预测更准确。

结论: 积分器式更新机制能显著降低偏差和扰动放大，是实现混沌系统稳定长期预测的关键架构特征。

局限 / 后续问题: 

中文摘要: 时间代理模型在计算成本极高的混沌动力系统预测中十分有效。多种深度神经网络架构可用于此目的。本文在统一的训练协议下比较了几种常用架构，旨在公平评估模型架构对长期预测稳定性的影响。实验针对三个问题展开：双摆、Kuramoto-Sivashinsky方程和Kolmogorov流。实验在匹配模型容量的条件下进行，同时对每个模型单独优化的情景也进行了分析。结果表明，在两种情景下，不同模型在长期推演中表现出类别性差异。为了进行具体量化，本文分析了逐步误差注入和扰动放大，使用了局部雅可比矩阵、相对单步偏差和有限时间Lyapunov增长等指标。此外还进行了吸引子分析，以评估学习模型对底层系统几何结构的复现能力。同时开展了消融实验，以分离连续更新架构中各组件的影响。结论是，具有积分器式更新的模型表现出更低的偏差和扰动放大，从而实现稳定的长期推演和更准确的预测。

英文原始摘要: Temporal surrogate models are effective for predicting chaotic dynamical systems where computational cost can be prohibitive. Several deep neural network architectures can be used for such purposes. In this work, a few commonly used architectures are compared using a common training protocol. The objective is to fairly assess the impact of model architectures for long-horizon prediction stability. Experiments are carried out for three problems, the double pendulum, the Kuramoto-Sivashinsky equations, and the Kolmogorov flow. The experiments are carried out with matching model capacity. Analysis is also carried out for a scenario where each model is individually optimized. It is observed that in both scenarios, the models exhibit categorical differences in long-horizon rollouts. For a concrete quantification, stepwise error injections and perturbation amplifications are analyzed using metrics such as local jacobian, relative one-step bias, and finite-time Lyapunov growth. Additionally, an attractor analysis is also conducted to assess how well the learned models replicate the underlying system geometry. An ablation study to isolate the impact of each component of a continuous-update architecture is also carried out. It is concluded that models that having integrator-like updates show lower bias and perturbation amplification yielding stable long-horizon rollout and more accurate predictions.

</details>

*Yan Yang*

主分类: 数值计算与科学计算

来源: arxiv

期刊: 

DOI: 

免费全文: [arXiv abstract](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.11258v1), [arXiv pdf](https://arxiv.org/pdf/2606.11258v1), [Google Scholar search](https://scholar.google.com/scholar?q=Loss+Landscape+Diagnosis+for+Gradient-Based+Gray-Scott+System+Inversion%3A+Disentangling+the+Roles+of+PINN+Components++free+full+text+pdf)

一句话结论: 直接通过PDE结构反向传播揭示损失地形病态，并厘清PINN各组件的分工。

研究问题: 反应扩散系统的梯度反演中，直接通过PDE结构本身进行反向传播这一最直接途径长期被回避，其失败的根本原因以及PINN中残差损失与神经网络各自的具体作用尚不明确。

主要创新点: 不使用代理模型或神经网络增强，直接通过展开的Gray-Scott模拟反向传播作为诊断探针，并将此最小化设置视为PINN的消融实验，从而明确厘清残差损失与神经网络各自的分工。


<details>
  <summary>更多信息</summary>

研究动机: 现有方法回避了通过PDE结构本身直接反向传播这一最直接途径，作者希望直接尝试该途径以诊断其失败原因，并借此理解PINN中各组件的实际作用与贡献。

主要方法: 将稳态损失通过展开的Gray-Scott模拟进行反向传播以恢复PDE参数，不使用任何代理或神经网络增强；直接绘制损失地形图以定位失败原因；将此最小化设置作为PINN的消融实验，逐一分析残差损失与神经网络的角色。

实验 / 验证: 在Gray-Scott反应扩散模型上，使用多种损失函数进行参数反演优化实验，并直接绘制损失地形以分析其几何结构与失败位置。

主要结果: 优化未能收敛；失败源于损失地形的几何病态——平坦高原无梯度信号，边界为与分岔边界对齐的陡峭悬崖，该结构在不同损失函数中反复出现。固定神经网络后，残差损失关于PDE参数为二次型，地形平滑，本身即可避免病态；神经网络无法修复不适定参数子空间，仅补全观测数据。

结论: PINN中残差损失通过隐式编码全部PDE动力学来避免地形病态，神经网络仅负责数据补全，二者分工此前未被明确揭示；这些发现为PINN类方法提供了具体设计启示，并给出了关于何时添加维度真正有用的启发性指导。

局限 / 后续问题: 

中文摘要: 反应扩散系统的基于梯度的反演通常通过代理模型或物理信息神经网络（PINN）来实现，而最直接的途径——通过PDE结构本身进行反向传播——在很大程度上被回避。我们将这一直接途径作为诊断探针，通过展开的Gray-Scott模拟反向传播稳态损失以恢复其参数，不使用任何代理或神经网络增强。优化未能收敛，通过直接绘制损失地形图，我们将失败定位在其几何结构中——没有梯度信号的平坦高原，其边界为与分岔边界对齐的陡峭悬崖——这一结构在不同的损失函数中反复出现，并且无论梯度如何路由到参数都会继承。将这一最小化设置视为对PINN的消融实验，我们厘清了各组件的作用：在神经网络固定的情况下，残差损失关于PDE参数是二次的，并产生平滑的地形，因此它本身就已经避免了病态，通过隐式编码所有初始条件下的完整PDE动力学。而神经网络本身无法修复不适定的参数子空间，因此仅用于补全观测数据——这一分工此前未被明确揭示。这些发现对PINN类方法具有具体的设计启示，并对何时添加维度真正有用提供了更广泛的启发性指导。

英文原始摘要: Gradient-based inversion of reaction-diffusion systems is typically approached via surrogate models or physics-informed neural networks (PINNs), while the most direct route, backpropagation through the PDE's structure itself, has largely been avoided. We pursue this direct route as a diagnostic probe, backpropagating a steady-state loss through unrolled Gray-Scott simulation to recover its parameters, with no surrogate or neural-network augmentation. Optimization fails to converge, and plotting the landscape directly locates the failure in its geometry -- flat plateaus with no gradient signal, bounded by sharp cliffs that align with bifurcation boundaries -- a structure that recurs across loss functions and is inherited however the gradients are routed to parameters. Reading this minimal setup as an ablation of PINN, we disentangle each component's role: with the neural network fixed, the residual loss is quadratic in the PDE parameters and yields a smooth landscape, so it alone already avoids the pathology, by implicitly encoding the full PDE dynamics across all initial conditions. The neural network, for its part, cannot repair an ill-posed parameter subspace, and so serves only to complete the observed data -- a division of labor not previously made explicit. These findings carry concrete design implications for PINN-type methods and a broader heuristic on when added dimensions actually help.

</details>

主分类: 数值计算与科学计算

一句话结论: 将SSM理论扩展至时滞微分方程，实现无需方程信息的数据驱动模型降阶。

研究问题: 非线性时滞微分方程系统的模型降阶问题，尤其是在底层DDE形式与时滞信息未知的情况下，如何获得可靠的低维预测模型。

主要创新点: 将谱子流形（SSM）理论扩展至时滞微分方程，提出一种纯数据驱动的SSM降阶算法，无需任何关于底层DDE形式或时滞数目与大小的先验信息，即可生成无时滞的低维ODE模型。


<details>
  <summary>更多信息</summary>

研究动机: 基于方程的SSM降阶方法依赖于对底层DDE的完整知识，而实际场景中方程形式与时滞信息往往不可获取，因此需要纯数据驱动的方法。

主要方法: 利用扩展至DDE的SSM理论，通过数据驱动算法在不变谱子流形上构建低维降阶模型，将含时滞系统转化为无时滞ODE模型，并进一步扩展至参数化降阶与非自治周期时滞系统。

实验 / 验证: 在标量单时滞DDE上对比方程驱动与数据驱动SSM降阶；对多个非线性时滞系统进行纯数据驱动降阶并在混沌动力学下验证预测能力；对含分布与离散时滞的系统进行参数化SSM降阶以捕获分岔；最后应用于具有反馈时滞和量化的控制系统实验数据。

主要结果: 数据驱动SSM降阶模型在混沌动力学下仍保持预测能力；参数化降阶能成功捕获含分布与离散时滞系统的分岔；扩展方法可应用于非自治周期时滞系统的实验数据。

结论: 扩展至DDE的SSM理论使得纯数据驱动的非线性时滞系统模型降阶成为可能，所得低维无时滞模型在包括混沌动力学和实验场景中均具有良好的预测能力。

局限 / 后续问题: 

中文摘要: 我们展示了最近将谱子流形（SSM）理论向时滞微分方程（DDE）的扩展如何实现对非线性时滞系统的数据驱动模型降阶。首先，使用一个具有单个离散时滞的标量DDE，我们比较了基于方程和基于数据驱动的SSM降阶，以说明后者的必要性。然后，我们使用相同的算法为若干非线性时滞系统获取了纯数据驱动的、SSM降阶的、无时滞ODE模型。我们的方法不需要关于底层DDE形式的信息，也不需要关于其包含的时滞数目和大小的信息。我们的SSM降阶低维模型即使在混沌动力学情形下仍保持预测能力。我们还展示了参数化SSM降阶在同时包含分布时滞和离散时滞的系统中捕获分岔的应用。最后，我们将时滞SSM降阶的理论基础扩展到具有周期时滞的非自治系统，并将这些结果应用于具有反馈时滞和量化的控制系统的实验数据。

英文原始摘要: We show how the recent extension of spectral submanifold (SSM) theory to delay differential equations (DDEs) enables data-driven model reduction of nonlinear delay systems. First, using a scalar DDE with a single discrete delay, we compare equation-based and data-driven SSM reductions, to illustrate the need for the latter. We then use the same algorithm to obtain purely data-driven, SSM-reduced, delay-free ODE models for several nonlinear delayed systems. Our approach requires no information about the form of the underlying DDE, or about the number and magnitude of the delays it contains. Our SSM-reduced, low-dimensional models remain predictive even for chaotic dynamics. We also illustrate the use of parametric SSM-reduction to capture bifurcations in systems with both distributed and discrete delays. Finally we extend the theoretical underpinning of delayed SSM-reductions to non-autonomous systems with periodic delays, and apply these results to experimental data from a control system with feedback delay and quantization.

</details>

*Nanxi Chen,Chuanjie Cui,Airong Chen,Sifan Wang,Rujin Ma*

DOI:

一句话结论: 系统研究PINO训练管线，发现CViT架构表现最优，PINN优化算法在PINO中同样有效。

研究问题: 如何高效且鲁棒地训练物理知情神经算子（PINO）这一问题目前理解不足，相比数据驱动神经算子和PINN的训练仍存在明显知识空白。

主要创新点: 系统性地研究了PINO训练管线的各个关键组件（架构、优化器、损失平衡、采样策略），并发现连续视觉Transformer（CViT）在多种PDE基准上表现最优；同时将PINN中的优化病理分析和缓解算法迁移到PINO场景。


<details>
  <summary>更多信息</summary>

研究动机: PINO结合了神经算子的泛化能力与物理知情学习的数据效率，但其训练方法学尚未成熟，缺乏对优化挑战的系统经验理解，需要为实践提供指导。

主要方法: 考察PINO训练管线中的架构设计、优化器选择、损失平衡和配置点采样策略；在五个参数化PDE系统上对比DeepONet、FNO和CViT三种骨干网络；分析梯度冲突和因果违反等优化病理，并应用PINN中的缓解算法；在不同数据量条件下对比物理知情与数据驱动训练。

实验 / 验证: 在五个不同的参数化PDE系统上评估三种算子骨干网络（DeepONet、FNO、CViT），比较不同架构、优化策略和采样方案的表现，并在多种数据条件下与纯数据驱动方法进行对比。

主要结果: CViT在所有基准上表现一致最强且最稳定；PINN训练中的梯度冲突和因果违反等病理在PINO中同样出现，且PINN的缓解算法在PINO中仍然有效；精心设计的物理知情训练管线可匹配甚至超越纯数据驱动神经算子。

结论: 该研究为PINO训练中的优化挑战提供了系统的经验理解，提出了一条高效鲁棒的物理知情算子学习实用管线，CViT为推荐骨干架构。

局限 / 后续问题: 

中文摘要: 物理知情神经算子（PINO）旨在通过将控制物理规律作为监督来学习偏微分方程的解算子，而非仅仅依赖成对的输入-输出仿真数据。通过将物理约束纳入训练目标，PINO结合了神经算子的跨实例泛化能力和物理知情学习的数据效率。尽管具有这一前景，如何高效且鲁棒地训练PINO仍然不如训练数据驱动的神经算子或物理知情神经网络（PINN）那样被充分理解。为弥合这一差距，我们研究了PINO训练管线的关键组件，包括架构设计、优化器选择、损失平衡和配置点采样策略。我们在五个不同的参数化偏微分方程系统上研究了三种具有代表性的算子骨干网络：深度算子网络（DeepONet）、傅里叶神经算子（FNO）和连续视觉Transformer（CViT）。我们的结果表明，CViT在所考虑的基准测试中始终提供强大且稳定的性能。除了架构之外，我们发现先前在PINN训练中识别出的若干优化病理现象在PINO中也会自然出现，包括梯度冲突和因果违反。我们还发现，为PINN开发的缓解算法在PINO设置中仍然有效。我们进一步在不同数据条件下比较了物理知情训练和数据驱动训练，揭示出精心设计的物理知情训练管线可以匹配，甚至在某些情况下超越纯数据驱动的神经算子。综上所述，这些发现为PINO训练中的优化挑战提供了系统的经验理解，并为高效鲁棒的物理知情算子学习提供了实用管线。代码和数据可在 https://github.com/NanxiiChen/PI-CViT 获取。

英文原始摘要: Physics-informed neural operators (PINOs) aim to learn solution operators for partial differential equations by using the governing physics as supervision, rather than relying solely on paired input-output simulation data. By incorporating physical constraints into the training objective, PINOs combine the cross-instance generalization of neural operators with the data efficiency of physics-informed learning. Despite this promise, how to train PINOs efficiently and robustly remains less well-understood than the training of either data-driven neural operators or physics-informed neural networks (PINNs). To bridge this gap, we examine key components of the PINO training pipeline, including architecture design, optimizer choice, loss balancing, and collocation-point sampling strategy. We study three representative operator backbones, Deep Operator Network (DeepONet), Fourier Neural Operator (FNO), and Continuous Vision Transformer (CViT), across five diverse parametric PDE systems. Our results show that CViT provides consistently strong and stable performance across the considered benchmarks. Beyond architecture, we find that several optimization pathologies previously identified in PINN training naturally arise in PINOs, including gradient conflicts and causal violation. We also find that mitigation algorithms developed for PINNs remain effective in the PINO setting. We further compare physics-informed and data-driven training under different data regimes, revealing that a carefully designed physics-informed training pipeline can match, and in some cases, outperform purely data-driven neural operators. Taken together, these findings provide a systematic empirical understanding of the optimization challenges in PINO training and inform a practical pipeline for efficient and robust physics-informed operator learning. Code and data are available at https://github.com/NanxiiChen/PI-CViT.

</details>


### [71] [Physical completion of the Navier-Stokes equations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.21357v2)
*Samuel L. Braunstein*

主分类: 数值计算与科学计算

来源: arxiv

期刊: 

DOI: 

免费全文: [arXiv abstract](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.21357v2), [arXiv pdf](https://arxiv.org/pdf/2605.21357v2), [Google Scholar search](https://scholar.google.com/scholar?q=Physical+completion+of+the+Navier-Stokes+equations++free+full+text+pdf)

一句话结论: 利用Poincaré引理的拓扑论证证明非线性Navier-Stokes的涨落-耗散关系无需线性化即严格成立，Cla.

研究问题: 不可压缩Navier-Stokes方程包含黏性耗散但无热噪声，此前涨落-耗散关系的所有推导均依赖线性化或结构性假设，无法严格适用于完整非线性动力学。GENERIC框架中的可逆/不可逆分解是被假定而非证明的。

主要创新点: 基于Poincaré引理的拓扑论证，证明非线性对流项是Hamilton性的并精确从Fokker-Planck平衡条件中消去，从而无需线性化即可导出完整非线性系统的涨落-耗散关系，严格证明了GENERIC框架所假定的可逆/不可逆分解。


<details>
  <summary>更多信息</summary>

研究动机: 此前涨落-耗散关系的推导需要线性化或结构性假设，GENERIC框架的可逆/不可逆分解缺乏严格证明。作者希望在不引入这些假设的前提下严格建立该关系，并揭示Clay千禧年奖难题困难的物理根源。

主要方法: 利用基于Poincaré引理的拓扑论证分析非线性对流项的Hamilton性质；通过Fokker-Planck平衡条件证明对流项精确消去；引入物理分子尺度谱截断，将系统表述为有限维非退化噪声随机微分方程，并构造约束Lyapunov函数分析全局适定性与收敛性。

实验 / 验证: 本文为理论与数学分析研究，无数值或物理实验。

主要结果: 线性化涨落导出的噪声对完整非线性系统精确成立；所得随机系统全局适定，具有唯一Gibbs平衡态并以指数速度收敛；GENERIC框架的可逆/不可逆分解得到严格证明而非仅作为假定。

结论: Clay千禧年奖难题的困难完全源于零温度和无穷谱分辨率两个理想化假设，任何物理流体均不满足这两个假设；引入物理分子尺度谱截断和热噪声后，系统全局适定且具有唯一平衡态。

局限 / 后续问题: 结果要求Poincaré引理在相空间上成立；引入了分子尺度谱截断将系统限制为有限维，与无截断的原始Navier-Stokes方程不同。

中文摘要: 不可压缩Navier-Stokes方程包含黏性耗散但不包含热噪声。我利用基于Poincaré引理的拓扑论证，证明可以在不需要以往所有推导所要求的线性化或结构性假设的情况下，导出完整非线性动力学的涨落-耗散关系。非线性对流项是Hamilton性的（保持能量和相空间体积不变），并且精确地从Fokker-Planck平衡条件中消去，因此从平衡态附近的线性化涨落导出的噪声实际上对完整非线性系统是精确的。这一结果证明而非假定了GENERIC框架所提出的可逆/不可逆分解，前提是Poincaré引理在相空间上成立。由此得到的具有物理分子尺度谱截断的随机系统是平凡全局适定的：一个具有非退化噪声和约束Lyapunov函数的有限维随机微分方程。它具有唯一的Gibbs平衡态并以指数速度收敛到该平衡态。Clay千禧年奖难题的困难完全源于两个理想化假设——零温度和无穷谱分辨率——而这两个假设在任何物理流体中都不满足。

英文原始摘要: The incompressible Navier-Stokes equations contain viscous dissipation but no thermal noise. I show, using a topological argument based on Poincaré's lemma, that the fluctuation-dissipation relation for the full nonlinear dynamics can be derived without the linearisation or structural assumptions that all previous derivations require. The nonlinear convective term is Hamiltonian (energy-preserving and phase-space-volume-preserving) and drops out of the Fokker-Planck equilibrium condition exactly, so the noise derived from linearised fluctuations near equilibrium is in fact exact for the full nonlinear system. This result proves, rather than assumes, the reversible/irreversible decomposition that the GENERIC framework postulates, provided Poincaré's lemma holds on the phase space. The resulting stochastic system, with a physical molecular-scale spectral cutoff, is trivially globally well-posed: a finite-dimensional stochastic differential equation with non-degenerate noise and a confining Lyapunov function. It has a unique Gibbs equilibrium and converges to it exponentially. The difficulty of the Clay Millennium Prize Problem arises entirely from two idealisations, zero temperature and infinite spectral resolution, neither of which is satisfied by any physical fluid.

</details>
